          "count": 56
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_fruits",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_fruits.json",
          "text": "A typical fruit tree.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Wild Fruits",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 56
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_fruits",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_fruits.json",
          "text": "Fruit trees start out at Saplings. Saplings will only start growing, placing their first piece of the tree, if it is not the dormant season for that fruit. The size of the finished tree is loosely determined by how many saplings are in the original sapling block. More saplings means a bigger tree. More saplings can be added to a single block through Splicing. To splice a sapling into another, just on it while holding a sapling and a Knife in your off hand.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Wild Fruits",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 56
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_fruits",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_fruits.json",
          "text": "To get saplings from a fruit tree, break the 'elbow' blocks (branch blocks that are attached to a block on one side and above) tree with an Axe. Saplings can also be placed on these 'elbow' sections, if they are not too high up in the tree. This allows one fruit tree to grow multiple fruits. Harvesting fruit is done with when the leaf block is bearing fruit. This will give one fruit, and revert the plant back to its growing stage, until it goes dormant for the winter.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Wild Fruits",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 56
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_fruits",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_fruits.json",
          "text": "Temperature: 5 - 25 °C Rainfall: 100 - 350mm Cherry trees grow in the months of January through March, start flowering in April and May, and bear fruit in June.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.6.text",
          "label": "Wild Fruits",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 56
        },

        {

          "text": "Temperature: 1 - 25 °C Rainfall: 100 - 280mm Red Apple trees grow from March to July, start flowering in August and September, and bear fruit in October.",

        {

        {

        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_fruits",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_fruits.json",
          "text": "Temperature: 17 - 35 °C Rainfall: 280 - 500mm Bananas are a special kind of fruit tree. They grow only vertically, lack leaves, and only fruit at the topmost block. Saplings are dropped from the flowering part of the plant. Once a banana plant is harvested, it dies, and will not produce any more fruit. It must be replanted in the spring.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.22.text",
          "label": "Wild Fruits",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 56
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_fruits",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_fruits.json",
          "text": "Banana Tree",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.22.title",
          "label": "Wild Fruits",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 56
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_fruits",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_fruits.json",
          "text": "An example banana tree.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.23.text",
          "label": "Wild Fruits",

        },
        {

          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_fruits.json",
          "text": "The fruiting calendar for fruit trees.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.24.text",
          "label": "Wild Fruits",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 56
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_fruits",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_fruits.json",
          "text": "Fruit Trees",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.24.title",
          "label": "Wild Fruits",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 56
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_fruits",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_fruits.json",
          "text": "Tall Bushes are fruit blocks that are able to grow in all directions, and spread. They do this by either growing directly upwards, up to three high, or placing canes on their sides, which can mature into full bush blocks. After a while, the bushes will stop spreading, and reach maturity. Harvesting these bushes with a sharp tool has a chance to drop a new bush. Bushes that are fully mature will always drop themselves.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.26.text",
          "label": "Wild Fruits",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 56
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_fruits",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_fruits.json",
          "text": "Tall Bushes",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.26.title",
          "label": "Wild Fruits",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 56
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_fruits",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_fruits.json",
          "text": "A wild tall bush.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.27.text",
          "label": "Wild Fruits",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 56
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_fruits",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_fruits.json",
          "text": "Tall bushes are able to spread when their canes have somewhere to take root. Practically, this means that they need a solid block under them to place a new bush on. Providing a flat, open area free of grass or other debris gives them the best chance to grow.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.28.text",
          "label": "Wild Fruits",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 56
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_fruits",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_fruits.json",
          "text": "Bushes, unlike fruit trees, take into account surrounding water blocks to determine their Hydration, unlike fruit trees, which only care about rainfall. Any full bush block can grow berries, which are harvestable with .",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.29.text",
          "label": "Wild Fruits",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 56
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_fruits",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_fruits.json",
          "text": "Temperature: 7 - 24 °C Rainfall: 24 - 100mm Blackberry bushes grow from February to May, start flowering in June and July, and bear fruit in August. They can be found in areas with few trees.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.30.text",
          "label": "Wild Fruits",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 56
        },
        {

      "id": "tfc:animal/husbandry",
      "label": "Animal Husbandry",

      "support": 40,
      "evidence": [
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry",

        }
      ],
      "semantic_evidence": [
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry.json",
          "text": "Animal Husbandry",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Animal Husbandry",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 40
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry.json",
          "text": "Livestock are animals that can be tamed and bred by the player. Livestock can be either male or female. For some animals, it is possible to tell their sex visually. For example, male pigs have tusks.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Animal Husbandry",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 40
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry.json",
          "text": "Livestock experience aging. They are born as babies, which are smaller and cannot provide things for the player. After a certain number of days, they grow into adult animals, which are able to do things like breed or produce milk. After they breed or are used enough times, animals become old, and are only useful for their meat.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Animal Husbandry",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 40
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry.json",
          "text": "This bull is old and cannot breed, so it has a faded coat and grey, unseeing eyes.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Animal Husbandry",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 40
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry.json",
          "text": "Livestock can be fed to raise familiarity. Each animal has foods that it prefers to eat. To feed an animal, and with food in your hand.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Animal Husbandry",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 40
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry.json",
          "text": "Livestock have a Familiarity Indicator that shows how familiar they are with you. Hold and look at the animal to reveal it.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Animal Husbandry",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 40
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry.json",
          "text": "Familiarity decays a little each day if not fed. Raising the familiarity enough prevents decay, indicated by the white outlined heart.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Animal Husbandry",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 40
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry.json",
          "text": "Adult livestock cannot be fully familiarized, indicated by the red outlined heart. Babies can reach 100% familiarity.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.6.text",
          "label": "Animal Husbandry",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 40
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry.json",
          "text": "Mammals are livestock that experience Pregnancy. Adult mammals that are above 30% familiarity and have been fed that day will mate, given that they are of opposite genders and near each other. The female animal will become pregnant, which causes it to have children a set number of days after fertilization. An example is Pigs.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.7.text",
          "label": "Animal Husbandry",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 40
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry.json",
          "text": "Wooly Animals are Mammals that can be Sheared if they are adults and familiar enough to you. Some examples are Sheep, Alpacas, and Musk Oxen.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.8.text",
          "label": "Animal Husbandry",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 40
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry.json",
          "text": "Dairy Animals are mammals that make Milk. Female dairy animals can be clicked with a bucket to obtain milk. Some examples are Goats, Cows, and Yaks.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.9.text",
          "label": "Animal Husbandry",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 40
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry.json",
          "text": "Nest Box",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.10.title",
          "label": "Animal Husbandry",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 40
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry.json",
          "text": "Oviparous Animals are not Mammals, and instead produce children by laying Eggs. They need a Nest Box to lay eggs, which they are capable of locating on their own. Some examples are Ducks, Quails, and Chickens.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.10.text",
          "label": "Animal Husbandry",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 40
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry.json",
          "text": "Eggs can be cooked or boiled for food. Male oviparous animals can fertilize females, which causes the next egg laid in the nest box to be fertilized. Fertilized eggs will have a tooltip with how long until they are ready to hatch.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.11.text",
          "label": "Animal Husbandry",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 40
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry.json",
          "text": "Equines are Mammals that can be ridden when tamed. They become rideable after reaching 15% familiarity.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.12.text",
          "label": "Animal Husbandry",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 40
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry.json",
          "text": "They need a Saddle to ride, which can be Knapped. This includes Mules, Donkeys, and Horses. Mules and Donkeys can hold any chest or barrel. If holding a barrel, while holding can be used to remove it. The same keys while holding a bucket can be used to drain fluid from the barrel. The next few pages will go over all livestock types.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.13.text",
          "label": "Animal Husbandry",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 40
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry.json",
          "text": "Pigs spawn in mild forests with temperature between -10 and 35°C, and at least 200mm of rainfall. They are Mammals with no special abilities. They will eat any food, even if it is rotten. They have 1-10 children, are pregnant for just 19 days, and reach adulthood in 80 days. They can have children 6 times.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.14.text",
          "label": "Animal Husbandry",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 40
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry.json",
          "text": "A pig.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.15.text",

          "text": "Cows spawn in most climates, between temperature -10 and 35°C, and at least 250mm of rainfall. They are Dairy Animals. They only eat grains, which may be rotten. They can have 1-2 children, are pregnant for 58 days, and reach adulthood in 192 days. They can have children 13 times, if they are never milked, or be milked 128 times, if they are never bred. They produce milk every day.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.16.text",
          "label": "Animal Husbandry",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 40

          "text": "Goats spawn in moderate climates, with temperature between -12 and 25°C, and at least 300mm of rainfall. They are Dairy Animals. They eat grains, fruits, and vegetables, which may be rotten. They can have 1-2 children, are pregnant for 32 days, and reach adulthood in 96 days.           "text": "Yaks spawn in cold climates, with temperature of at most -11°C, and at least 100mm of rainfall. They are Dairy Animals. They eat only fresh grains. They produce milk once a day.",

          "text": "Alpacas spawn in moderate climates, with temperature between -8 and 20°C, and at least 250mm of rainfall. They are Wooly Animals. They eat grains and fruits.           "text": "Sheep spawn in drier climates, with temperature between 0 and 35°C, and between 70 and 300mm of rainfall. They are Wooly Animals. They eat grains.           "text": "Chickens spawn in warm forests, with temperature of at least 14°C, and at least 225mm of rainfall. They are Oviparous Animals.           "text": "Ducks spawn in most plains, with temperature between -25 and 30°C, and at least 100mm of rainfall. They are Oviparous Animals.           "text": "Quails spawn in colder climates, with temperature between -15 and 15°C, and at least 200mm of rainfall. They are Oviparous Animals.           "text": "Donkeys spawn in wetter plains, with temperature of at least -15°C, and between 130 and 400mm of rainfall. They are a kind of Equine that can carry a chest. They eat grains and fruits. They have 1 child, are pregnant for 19 days, and reach adulthood in 80 days. They can have children 6 times.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.34.text",

          "text": "Mules spawn in plains with temperature of at least -15°C, and between 130 and 400mm of rainfall. They are a kind of Equine that can carry a chest and are the always-male product of a horse and a donkey. They eat grains and fruits. They reach adulthood in 80 days.",

          "text": "Horses spawn in plains with temperature of at least -15°C, and between 130 and 400mm of rainfall. They are a kind of Equine. They eat grains and fruits. They have 1 child, are pregnant for 19 days, and reach adulthood in 80 days. They can have children 6 times.",

        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel.json",
          "text": "Better Fuel",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Better Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel.json",
          "text": "Cryo Freezer",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.title",
          "label": "Better Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel.json",
          "text": "Fuel is hard to get; it requires venturing out to the ocean, looking for oil spouts. The Cryo Freezer introduces a new way to obtain a better fuel - Cryo Fuel. Cryo Fuel is much more efficient than refined fuel, allowing you to launch a rocket with just one bucket.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Better Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel.json",
          "text": "It's created by collecting ice shards, which are found as an ore underground on cold planets. The ice shard then needs to be inserted into a Cryo Freezer, and it will convert to 25 mB of Cryo Fuel. Alternatively, you can convert ice, packed ice and blue ice to fuel. Once you've made at least two buckets (one for a launch and the other for a return trip), place the buckets in the rocket fuel slot.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Better Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel.json",
          "text": "Cryo Fuel",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.title",
          "label": "Better Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel.json",
          "text": "In addition to being an excellent fuel, cryo fuel has some other features. When placed, it will damage anything inside, similar to lava. It also freezes water, allowing for an infinite ice source.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Better Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/detection",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/detection.json",
          "text": "Oxygen Sensor",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.title",
          "label": "Detection",
          "item_ref_count": 3,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/detection",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/detection.json",
          "text": "The oxygen sensor is a block that will emit a redstone signal when it detects oxygen. You can shift-right-click it to invert the signal. Despite its name, you can also change its detection type by right-clicking with a wrench. It has the option to detect oxygen, safe temperature, and normal gravity.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Detection",
          "item_ref_count": 3,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/energizer",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/energizer.json",
          "text": "The energizer is a machine capable of powering items and storing large amounts of energy. To charge an item, power the energizer and then right-click on the energizer with that item. It stores 2 million energy, making it capable of charging machines for a very long time. It also retains its energy when broken.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Energizer",
          "item_ref_count": 3,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/flags",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/flags.json",
          "text": "Flags are decorative blocks available in 16 colors. By default, when placed, they will display the face of the flag owner. They can be configured to display a custom image from a URL by right-clicking and entering the URL in the GUI. This will display the internet image onto the flag.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Flags",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",

        {

          "text": "Fluid pipes are similar to cables but with fluid transfer instead of energy transfer. To use them, craft some fluid pipes and a wrench. Then, right-click on one of the pipes with the wrench to cycle through the pipe's modes: 'none,' 'normal,' 'insert,' and 'extract.'",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Fluid Pipes",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/fluid_pipes",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/fluid_pipes.json",
          "text": "Pipes with 'insert' set will receive fluid from the network, while pipes with 'extract' set will pump fluid into the network.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Fluid Pipes",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/fluid_pipes",

          "text": "Like cables, fluid pipes also have a duct block for transfering fluids safely in and out of sealed structures.",

          "label": "Fluid Pipes",
          "item_ref_count": 4,

      "semantic_evidence": [
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/papermaking",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/papermaking.json",
          "text": "Paper is either made from the processed stalk of the Papyrus crop, from Animal Hides, or from a lengthy process using specific types of Wood. Paper is useful for written materials like Books and Maps.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Papermaking",
          "item_ref_count": 20,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 26
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/papermaking",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/papermaking.json",
          "text": "Papyrus must first be cut into strips with a Knife",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Papermaking",
          "item_ref_count": 20,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 26
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/papermaking",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/papermaking.json",
          "text": "Then, papyrus strips are soaked in a Barrel of Water.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Papermaking",
          "item_ref_count": 20,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 26
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/papermaking",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/papermaking.json",
          "text": "Then, soaked papyrus strips are woven together in a loom to make Unrefined Paper. Finally, it must be placed on a log and Scraped to make Paper.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Papermaking",
          "item_ref_count": 20,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 26
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/papermaking",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/papermaking.json",
          "text": "Flip to the next page for information on papermaking via the parchment process.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.7.text",
          "label": "Papermaking",
          "item_ref_count": 20,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 26
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/papermaking",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/papermaking.json",
          "text": "Parchment Paper starts with a scraped hide. Review the leather making chapter to learn how to make it. Parchment requires treatment with a few different items. First, Pumice is needed. Pumice is found on the ground near Volcanoes, or from Sluicing or Panning ore deposits with Andesite, Rhyolite, or Dacite in them.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.8.text",
          "label": "Papermaking",
          "item_ref_count": 20,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 26
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/papermaking",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/papermaking.json",
          "text": "A Pumice rock placed on the ground.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.9.text",
          "label": "Papermaking",
          "item_ref_count": 20,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 26
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/papermaking",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/papermaking.json",
          "text": "Crafting pumice, a hammer, and scraped hide gives sections of Treated Hide.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.10.text",
          "label": "Papermaking",
          "item_ref_count": 20,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 26
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/papermaking",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/papermaking.json",
          "text": "Treated hide, lime powder, flour, and a fresh egg will complete the treatment process and yield usable paper.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.11.text",
          "label": "Papermaking",
          "item_ref_count": 20,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 26
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/papermaking",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/papermaking.json",
          "text": "Creating Paper from Wood is an ancient process that requires processing of sturdy types of wood into sheets, which are broken down into a pulp, and then pressed, dried and scraped to make paper. Note that softer types of wood are not suitable for making paper",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.12.text",
          "label": "Papermaking",
          "item_ref_count": 20,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 26
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/papermaking",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/papermaking.json",
          "text": "Paper from Wood",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.12.title",
          "label": "Papermaking",
          "item_ref_count": 20,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 26
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/papermaking",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/papermaking.json",
          "text": "First you need to find Hardwood: Acacia Ash Aspen Birch Blackwood Chestnut Hickory Maple Oak Rosewood Sycamore",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.13.text",
          "label": "Papermaking",
          "item_ref_count": 20,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 26
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/papermaking",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/papermaking.json",
          "text": "Stripped Hardwood",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.14.title",
          "label": "Papermaking",
          "item_ref_count": 20,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 26
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/papermaking",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/papermaking.json",
          "text": "The process begins by stripping the Hardwood's bark off to expose the internal fibers of the log, You can strip the log by placing it down and right clicking it with an axe",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.14.text",
          "label": "Papermaking",
          "item_ref_count": 20,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 26
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/papermaking",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/papermaking.json",
          "text": "Hardwood Strip",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.15.title",
          "label": "Papermaking",
          "item_ref_count": 20,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 26
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/papermaking",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/papermaking.json",
          "text": "Next, the stripped wood is shaven with an axe to obtain Hardwood Strips. This can be done using an Axe or more sophisticated methods",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.15.text",
          "label": "Papermaking",
          "item_ref_count": 20,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 26
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/papermaking",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/papermaking.json",
          "text": "Soaked Hardwood Strip",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.16.title",
          "label": "Papermaking",
          "item_ref_count": 20,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 26
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/papermaking",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/papermaking.json",
          "text": "Then, the individual strips are soaked in a Barrel of Water. This debilitates the structural integrity of the wood so it can be broken down further",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.16.text",
          "label": "Papermaking",
          "item_ref_count": 20,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 26
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/papermaking",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/papermaking.json",
          "text": "Hardwood Pulp",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.17.title",
          "label": "Papermaking",
          "item_ref_count": 20,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 26
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/papermaking",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/papermaking.json",
          "text": "Afterwards, utilize the Quern to break down the soaked strips into Hardwood Pulp",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.17.text",
          "label": "Papermaking",
          "item_ref_count": 20,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 26
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/papermaking",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/papermaking.json",
          "text": "Piling up the Pulp",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.18.title",
          "label": "Papermaking",
          "item_ref_count": 20,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 26
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/papermaking",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/papermaking.json",
          "text": "While the quern gives you small piles of pulp, you'll want to combine them together in a Workbench. More sophisticated methods of crushing the strips yield higher amounts of pulp",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.18.text",
          "label": "Papermaking",
          "item_ref_count": 20,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 26
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/papermaking",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/papermaking.json",
          "text": "Breaking down the Pulp",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.19.title",
          "label": "Papermaking",
          "item_ref_count": 20,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 26
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/papermaking",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/papermaking.json",
          "text": "Before the pulp can be processed further into paper, it needs to be broken down further by Boiling it with Lye in a Vat or a Pot.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.19.text",
          "label": "Papermaking",
          "item_ref_count": 20,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 26
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/papermaking",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/papermaking.json",
          "text": "Pressing the Pulp",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.21.title",
          "label": "Papermaking",
          "item_ref_count": 20,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 26
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/papermaking",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/papermaking.json",
          "text": "After broken down by boiling it with Lye, the Thermochemically Treated Hardwood Pulp can be pressed down into a sheet of Soaked Unrefined Paper",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.21.text",
          "label": "Papermaking",
          "item_ref_count": 20,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 26
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/papermaking",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/papermaking.json",
          "text": "You can utilize an Anvil to beat down the pulp into a sheet",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.22.text",
          "label": "Papermaking",
          "item_ref_count": 20,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 26
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/papermaking",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/papermaking.json",
          "text": "Stomping in Barrel",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.23.title",
          "label": "Papermaking",
          "item_ref_count": 20,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 26
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/papermaking",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/papermaking.json",
          "text": "Alternatively, you can stomp the Thermochemically Treated Hardwood Pulp in a Stomping Barrel",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.23.text",
          "label": "Papermaking",
          "item_ref_count": 20,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 26
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/papermaking",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/papermaking.json",
          "text": "The final step is Drying the Soaked Unrefined Paper into Unrefined Paper, Which then can be Scraped into Paper as shown before.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.24.text",
          "label": "Papermaking",
          "item_ref_count": 20,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 26
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/papermaking",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/papermaking.json",
          "text": "Cutting Steps...?",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.25.title",
          "label": "Papermaking",
          "item_ref_count": 20,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 26
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/papermaking",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/papermaking.json",
          "text": "Advancing in the Tech-Tree will allow you to create paper from wood with less overall steps, check EMI for the different approaches you can take for making paper with wood.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.25.text",
          "label": "Papermaking",

          "text": "Paper is either made from the processed stalk of the Papyrus crop, or from Animal Hides. Paper is useful for written materials like Books and Maps.",

          "label": "Papermaking",

          "recipe_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/papermaking",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/papermaking.json",
          "text": "Then, papyrus strips are soaked in a Barrel of Water.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Papermaking",
          "item_ref_count": 11,
          "recipe_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/papermaking",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/papermaking.json",
          "text": "Then, soaked papyrus strips are woven together in a loom to make Unrefined Paper. Finally, it must be placed on a log and Scraped to make Paper.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Papermaking",
          "item_ref_count": 11,
          "recipe_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/papermaking",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/papermaking.json",
          "text": "Flip to the next page for information on papermaking via the parchment process.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.7.text",
          "label": "Papermaking",
          "item_ref_count": 11,
          "recipe_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/papermaking",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/papermaking.json",
          "text": "Parchment Paper starts with a scraped hide. Review the leather making chapter to learn how to make it. Parchment requires treatment with a few different items. First, Pumice is needed. Pumice is found on the ground near Volcanoes, or from Sluicing or Panning ore deposits with Andesite, Rhyolite, or Dacite in them.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.8.text",
          "label": "Papermaking",
          "item_ref_count": 11,
          "recipe_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/papermaking",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/papermaking.json",
          "text": "A Pumice rock placed on the ground.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.9.text",
          "label": "Papermaking",
          "item_ref_count": 11,
          "recipe_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/papermaking",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/papermaking.json",
          "text": "Crafting pumice, a hammer, and scraped hide gives sections of Treated Hide.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.10.text",
          "label": "Papermaking",
          "item_ref_count": 11,
          "recipe_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/papermaking",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/papermaking.json",
          "text": "Treated hide, lime powder, flour, and a fresh egg will complete the treatment process and yield usable paper.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.11.text",
          "label": "Papermaking",
          "item_ref_count": 11,
          "recipe_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "runtime_item",
          "id": "minecraft:book",
          "source": "runtime-items",
          "text": "Burns at Hot٭٭٭٭ for 00:09",
          "key": "runtime-tooltip",
          "label": "Book",

        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel.json",
          "text": "Better Fuel",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Better Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel.json",
          "text": "Cryo Freezer",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.title",
          "label": "Better Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel.json",
          "text": "Fuel is hard to get; it requires venturing out to the ocean, looking for oil spouts. The Cryo Freezer introduces a new way to obtain a better fuel - Cryo Fuel. Cryo Fuel is much more efficient than refined fuel, allowing you to launch a rocket with just one bucket.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Better Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel.json",
          "text": "It's created by collecting ice shards, which are found as an ore underground on cold planets. The ice shard then needs to be inserted into a Cryo Freezer, and it will convert to 25 mB of Cryo Fuel. Alternatively, you can convert ice, packed ice and blue ice to fuel. Once you've made at least two buckets (one for a launch and the other for a return trip), place the buckets in the rocket fuel slot.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Better Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel.json",
          "text": "Cryo Fuel",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.title",
          "label": "Better Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel.json",
          "text": "In addition to being an excellent fuel, cryo fuel has some other features. When placed, it will damage anything inside, similar to lava. It also freezes water, allowing for an infinite ice source.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Better Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/detection",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/detection.json",
          "text": "Oxygen Sensor",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.title",
          "label": "Detection",
          "item_ref_count": 3,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/detection",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/detection.json",
          "text": "The oxygen sensor is a block that will emit a redstone signal when it detects oxygen. You can shift-right-click it to invert the signal. Despite its name, you can also change its detection type by right-clicking with a wrench. It has the option to detect oxygen, safe temperature, and normal gravity.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Detection",
          "item_ref_count": 3,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/energizer",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/energizer.json",
          "text": "The energizer is a machine capable of powering items and storing large amounts of energy. To charge an item, power the energizer and then right-click on the energizer with that item. It stores 2 million energy, making it capable of charging machines for a very long time. It also retains its energy when broken.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Energizer",
          "item_ref_count": 3,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/flags",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/flags.json",
          "text": "Flags are decorative blocks available in 16 colors. By default, when placed, they will display the face of the flag owner. They can be configured to display a custom image from a URL by right-clicking and entering the URL in the GUI. This will display the internet image onto the flag.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Flags",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/fluid_pipes",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/fluid_pipes.json",
          "text": "Fluid Pipes",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Fluid Pipes",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/fluid_pipes",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/fluid_pipes.json",
          "text": "Fluid Pipes",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.title",
          "label": "Fluid Pipes",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/fluid_pipes",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/fluid_pipes.json",
          "text": "Fluid pipes are similar to cables but with fluid transfer instead of energy transfer. To use them, craft some fluid pipes and a wrench. Then, right-click on one of the pipes with the wrench to cycle through the pipe's modes: 'none,' 'normal,' 'insert,' and 'extract.'",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Fluid Pipes",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/fluid_pipes",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/fluid_pipes.json",
          "text": "Pipes with 'insert' set will receive fluid from the network, while pipes with 'extract' set will pump fluid into the network.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Fluid Pipes",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/fluid_pipes",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/fluid_pipes.json",
          "text": "Like cables, fluid pipes also have a duct block for transfering fluids safely in and out of sealed structures.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Fluid Pipes",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/industrial_lamps",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/industrial_lamps.json",

        {

          "text": "Industrial lamps are available in all 16 colors, and in small and large sizes. By default, these will all emit normal white light; however, if you have Shimmer, each light will emit its respective color.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Industrial Lamps",

        {

        },
        {

          "source": "file:minecraft/lang-overlays",
          "text": "The stationary contact now turns into an Elevator Contact",

        },
        {

          "id": "create:ponder/elevator_pulley",
          "source": "file:minecraft/lang-overlays",
          "text": "Elevator Contacts represent a 'floor' and can be configured",

          "label": "Using the Elevator Pulley",
          "count": 19
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "create:ponder/elevator_pulley",
          "source": "jar:create-1.20.1-6.0.8.jar!assets/*/lang/en_us.json",
          "text": "Using the Elevator Pulley",

        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "create:ponder/elevator_pulley",
          "source": "jar:create-1.20.1-6.0.8.jar!assets/*/lang/en_us.json",
          "text": "Elevator Pulleys can move structures vertically between marked locations",
          "key": "lang-ponder:create.ponder.elevator_pulley.text_1",
          "label": "Using the Elevator Pulley",
          "count": 19
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "create:ponder/elevator_pulley",
          "source": "jar:create-1.20.1-6.0.8.jar!assets/*/lang/en_us.json",
          "text": "Any redstone contact sharing this column will be converted",
          "key": "lang-ponder:create.ponder.elevator_pulley.text_10",
          "label": "Using the Elevator Pulley",
          "count": 19
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "create:ponder/elevator_pulley",
          "source": "jar:create-1.20.1-6.0.8.jar!assets/*/lang/en_us.json",
          "text": "Supply a redstone pulse to call the elevator to the contact",
          "key": "lang-ponder:create.ponder.elevator_pulley.text_11",
          "label": "Using the Elevator Pulley",
          "count": 19
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "create:ponder/elevator_pulley",
          "source": "jar:create-1.20.1-6.0.8.jar!assets/*/lang/en_us.json",
          "text": "The movement speed depends on the rotation input on the pulley",
          "key": "lang-ponder:create.ponder.elevator_pulley.text_12",
          "label": "Using the Elevator Pulley",
          "count": 19
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "create:ponder/elevator_pulley",
          "source": "jar:create-1.20.1-6.0.8.jar!assets/*/lang/en_us.json",
          "text": "Scroll and click on the controls block to choose a floor while on-board",
          "key": "lang-ponder:create.ponder.elevator_pulley.text_13",
          "label": "Using the Elevator Pulley",
          "count": 19
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "create:ponder/elevator_pulley",
          "source": "jar:create-1.20.1-6.0.8.jar!assets/*/lang/en_us.json",
          "text": "Right-Clicking the assembled pulley will turn the cabin back into blocks",
          "key": "lang-ponder:create.ponder.elevator_pulley.text_14",
          "label": "Using the Elevator Pulley",
          "count": 19
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "create:ponder/elevator_pulley",
          "source": "jar:create-1.20.1-6.0.8.jar!assets/*/lang/en_us.json",
          "text": "Sliding doors attached to the cabin will open and close automatically",
          "key": "lang-ponder:create.ponder.elevator_pulley.text_15",
          "label": "Using the Elevator Pulley",
          "count": 19
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "create:ponder/elevator_pulley",
          "source": "jar:create-1.20.1-6.0.8.jar!assets/*/lang/en_us.json",

          "key": "lang-ponder:create.ponder.elevator_pulley.text_16",
          "label": "Using the Elevator Pulley",
          "count": 19
        },
        {

          "source": "jar:create-1.20.1-6.0.8.jar!assets/*/lang/en_us.json",

      "id": "create:automated/recipes/with/factory/gauges",

      "semantic_evidence": [
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "create:ponder/factory_gauge_recipe",
          "source": "file:minecraft/lang-overlays",
          "text": "Automated Recipes with Factory Gauges",
          "key": "lang-ponder:create.ponder.factory_gauge_recipe.header",
          "label": "Automated Recipes with Factory Gauges",
          "count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "create:ponder/factory_gauge_recipe",
          "source": "file:minecraft/lang-overlays",
          "text": "Whenever gauges are not placed on a packager...",
          "key": "lang-ponder:create.ponder.factory_gauge_recipe.text_1",
          "label": "Automated Recipes with Factory Gauges",
          "count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "create:ponder/factory_gauge_recipe",
          "source": "file:minecraft/lang-overlays",
          "text": "Specify the address that ingredients should be sent to",
          "key": "lang-ponder:create.ponder.factory_gauge_recipe.text_10",
          "label": "Automated Recipes with Factory Gauges",
          "count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "create:ponder/factory_gauge_recipe",
          "source": "file:minecraft/lang-overlays",
          "text": "The target amount to maintain can now be set by holding Right-click on the gauge",
          "key": "lang-ponder:create.ponder.factory_gauge_recipe.text_11",
          "label": "Automated Recipes with Factory Gauges",
          "count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "create:ponder/factory_gauge_recipe",
          "source": "file:minecraft/lang-overlays",
          "text": "Whenever the network has fewer items than the amount...",
          "key": "lang-ponder:create.ponder.factory_gauge_recipe.text_12",
          "label": "Automated Recipes with Factory Gauges",
          "count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "create:ponder/factory_gauge_recipe",
          "source": "file:minecraft/lang-overlays",
          "text": "...it will send new ingredients to the specified address",
          "key": "lang-ponder:create.ponder.factory_gauge_recipe.text_13",
          "label": "Automated Recipes with Factory Gauges",
          "count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "create:ponder/factory_gauge_recipe",
          "source": "file:minecraft/lang-overlays",
          "text": "The outputs then need to return to any of the linked inventories",
          "key": "lang-ponder:create.ponder.factory_gauge_recipe.text_14",
          "label": "Automated Recipes with Factory Gauges",
          "count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "create:ponder/factory_gauge_recipe",
          "source": "file:minecraft/lang-overlays",
          "text": "Green connections indicate that the target amount has been reached",
          "key": "lang-ponder:create.ponder.factory_gauge_recipe.text_15",
          "label": "Automated Recipes with Factory Gauges",
          "count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "create:ponder/factory_gauge_recipe",
          "source": "file:minecraft/lang-overlays",
          "text": "The board of gauges can expand to include more recipe steps",
          "key": "lang-ponder:create.ponder.factory_gauge_recipe.text_16",
          "label": "Automated Recipes with Factory Gauges",
          "count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "create:ponder/factory_gauge_recipe",
          "source": "file:minecraft/lang-overlays",
          "text": "Each gauge maintains the stock level of its item independently",
          "key": "lang-ponder:create.ponder.factory_gauge_recipe.text_17",
          "label": "Automated Recipes with Factory Gauges",
          "count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "create:ponder/factory_gauge_recipe",
          "source": "file:minecraft/lang-overlays",
          "text": "They will instead monitor stock levels of all linked inventories",
          "key": "lang-ponder:create.ponder.factory_gauge_recipe.text_2",
          "label": "Automated Recipes with Factory Gauges",
          "count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "create:ponder/factory_gauge_recipe",
          "source": "file:minecraft/lang-overlays",
          "text": "Right-click it with the item that should be monitored",
          "key": "lang-ponder:create.ponder.factory_gauge_recipe.text_3",
          "label": "Automated Recipes with Factory Gauges",
          "count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "create:ponder/factory_gauge_recipe",
          "source": "file:minecraft/lang-overlays",
          "text": "It will now display the total amount present on the network",
          "key": "lang-ponder:create.ponder.factory_gauge_recipe.text_4",
          "label": "Automated Recipes with Factory Gauges",
          "count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "create:ponder/factory_gauge_recipe",
          "source": "file:minecraft/lang-overlays",
          "text": "The gauge can replenish stock levels by sending other items to be processed",
          "key": "lang-ponder:create.ponder.factory_gauge_recipe.text_5",
          "label": "Automated Recipes with Factory Gauges",
          "count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "create:ponder/factory_gauge_recipe",
          "source": "file:minecraft/lang-overlays",
          "text": "First, add the required ingredients as new factory gauges",
          "key": "lang-ponder:create.ponder.factory_gauge_recipe.text_6",
          "label": "Automated Recipes with Factory Gauges",
          "count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "create:ponder/factory_gauge_recipe",
          "source": "file:minecraft/lang-overlays",
          "text": "From the target's UI, new connections can be made",
          "key": "lang-ponder:create.ponder.factory_gauge_recipe.text_7",
          "label": "Automated Recipes with Factory Gauges",
          "count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "create:ponder/factory_gauge_recipe",
          "source": "file:minecraft/lang-overlays",
          "text": "For aesthetics, input panels can be wrenched to change the pathing",
          "key": "lang-ponder:create.ponder.factory_gauge_recipe.text_8",
          "label": "Automated Recipes with Factory Gauges",
          "count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "create:ponder/factory_gauge_recipe",
          "source": "file:minecraft/lang-overlays",
          "text": "In the UI, review the inputs and specify how much of the output gets made per batch",
          "key": "lang-ponder:create.ponder.factory_gauge_recipe.text_9",
          "label": "Automated Recipes with Factory Gauges",
          "count": 18
        },
