      "semantic_evidence": [
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna.json",
          "text": "Extraterrestrial Fauna",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Extraterrestrial Fauna",
          "item_ref_count": 27,
          "count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna.json",
          "text": "Extraterrestrial Fauna",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.title",
          "label": "Extraterrestrial Fauna",
          "item_ref_count": 27,
          "count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna.json",
          "text": "Space is home to many other kinds of animals than those you'd find on Earth. A few animals are hostile and will attack the player, but most are passive. Only a few of them are domesticatable. Animals you find on other worlds can be divided into two categories: hot and cold. Animals can only survive on planets that either have a similar climate to where they came from, or on temperate planets like Earth.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Extraterrestrial Fauna",
          "item_ref_count": 27,
          "count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna.json",
          "text": "Space animals do not require an oxygenated environment, but it will make the area count as \"temperate\" for their survival. For example, if you bring a cold-dwelling animal (like a Sniffer) to a hot planet (like Venus), it will catch on fire, unless it's inside an oxygenated environment. This makes Earth the ideal place to build a zoo, if you'd like. You can transport animals between planets using AE2's Spatial Storage system.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Extraterrestrial Fauna",
          "item_ref_count": 27,
          "count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna.json",
          "text": "Moon Animals",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.title",
          "label": "Extraterrestrial Fauna",
          "item_ref_count": 27,
          "count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna.json",
          "text": "The Moon may be pretty barren, but it still has a few animals that call it home. It's considered a Cold world despite the pretty extreme temperature swings.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Extraterrestrial Fauna",
          "item_ref_count": 27,
          "count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna.json",
          "text": "Moon Rabbit",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.name",
          "label": "Extraterrestrial Fauna",
          "item_ref_count": 27,
          "count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna.json",
          "text": "Besides their coloring, Moon Rabbits have identical stats to Earth rabbits. They also eat Chorus Fruit.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Extraterrestrial Fauna",
          "item_ref_count": 27,
          "count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna.json",
          "text": "Limpets are a type of snail with ore on their shell. You can obtain the ore with any mining tool. They are passive and can be eaten.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Extraterrestrial Fauna",
          "item_ref_count": 27,
          "count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna.json",
          "text": "Star Crawler",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.name",
          "label": "Extraterrestrial Fauna",
          "item_ref_count": 27,
          "count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna.json",
          "text": "Star Crawlers are a type of starfish. They are hostile and can be eaten.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Extraterrestrial Fauna",
          "item_ref_count": 27,
          "count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna.json",
          "text": "Birts are a type of bird? They lay eggs in their nests, are passive unless attacked, and can be eaten. Their eggs can be thrown to stun targets.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.6.text",
          "label": "Extraterrestrial Fauna",
          "item_ref_count": 27,
          "count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna.json",
          "text": "Mars Animals",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.8.title",
          "label": "Extraterrestrial Fauna",
          "item_ref_count": 27,
          "count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna.json",
          "text": "Mars is the planet with an environment closest to Earth's, featuring a wide variety of animals to interact with and crops to feed them (and yourself) with... outside of the huge desert, where almost nothing lives. Mars is a Cold planet.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.8.text",
          "label": "Extraterrestrial Fauna",
          "item_ref_count": 27,
          "count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna.json",
          "text": "The Sandworm",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.9.title",
          "label": "Extraterrestrial Fauna",
          "item_ref_count": 27,
          "count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna.json",
          "text": "Sandworms are alerted by player footsteps. They are the reason almost nothing else lives in the desert.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.9.text",
          "label": "Extraterrestrial Fauna",
          "item_ref_count": 27,
          "count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna.json",
          "text": "Sniffers are domesticatable animals found between -30 and -102°C. They require a Large Nest to lay their eggs and can be shorn for Sniffer Tufts. Their eggs can be cooked or extracted for Cream. They eat any Mars crop.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.10.text",
          "label": "Extraterrestrial Fauna",
          "item_ref_count": 27,
          "count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna.json",
          "text": "A sniffer.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.11.text",
          "label": "Extraterrestrial Fauna",
          "item_ref_count": 27,
          "count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna.json",
          "text": "Wraptors are domesticatable animals found between -15 and -100°C. They require a Large Nest to lay their eggs and can be shorn for Wraptor Feathers. Their eggs can be cooked or extracted for Wraptor Sugar. They eat any Mars crop.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.12.text",
          "label": "Extraterrestrial Fauna",
          "item_ref_count": 27,
          "count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna.json",
          "text": "A wraptor.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.13.text",
          "label": "Extraterrestrial Fauna",
          "item_ref_count": 27,
          "count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna.json",
          "text": "Glacian Sheep",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.14.title",
          "label": "Extraterrestrial Fauna",
          "item_ref_count": 27,
          "count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna.json",
          "text": "Preferring extremely cold environments below -108°C, Glacian Sheep are domesticatable animals. They can be shorn for Glacian Wool, an amazing insulator. They eat any Mars crop.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.14.text",
          "label": "Extraterrestrial Fauna",
          "item_ref_count": 27,
          "count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna.json",
          "text": "A glacian ram/ewe.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.15.text",
          "label": "Extraterrestrial Fauna",
          "item_ref_count": 27,
          "count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna.json",
          "text": "Snatchers are passive animals that like to steal your stuff! Attack them to retrieve your items, or feed them Raw Fish to tame them. Once tamed, you can carry them on your back and equip them with a wooden chest. Press to throw them, and they will steal the contents of a nearby chest and bring it to you.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.16.text",
          "label": "Extraterrestrial Fauna",
          "item_ref_count": 27,
          "count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna.json",
          "text": "A snatcher.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.17.text",
          "label": "Extraterrestrial Fauna",
          "item_ref_count": 27,
          "count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna.json",
          "text": "Chargers are semipassive animals. You can tame them with any Mars crop to use them as a combat land mount. Press for a headbutt attack, or press and hold Spacebar for a charge attack. They can also be equipped with dyeable armor.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.18.text",
          "label": "Extraterrestrial Fauna",
          "item_ref_count": 27,
          "count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna.json",
          "text": "Chargers have a natural rivalry with Raiders. They will attack each other on sight.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.19.text",
          "label": "Extraterrestrial Fauna",
          "item_ref_count": 27,
          "count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna.json",
          "text": "Surfers are semiaquatic passive animals. You can tame them with Raw Fish to ride them underwater. They can also be eaten.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.20.text",
          "label": "Extraterrestrial Fauna",
          "item_ref_count": 27,
          "count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna.json",
          "text": "Soarers are passive animals found above -106°C. You can tame them with Raw Fish to use them as a flying mount. They can also be eaten.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.21.text",
          "label": "Extraterrestrial Fauna",
          "item_ref_count": 27,
          "count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna.json",
          "text": "A top predator, Eaters are hostile animals found above -109°C. If you can manage to take one down, they can be eaten.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.22.text",
          "label": "Extraterrestrial Fauna",
          "item_ref_count": 27,
          "count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna.json",
          "text": "Crunchers are territorial miniboss predators. Feed them meat to stop their regeneration. They can also be eaten.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.23.text",
          "label": "Extraterrestrial Fauna",
          "item_ref_count": 27,
          "count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna.json",
          "text": "Despite their name, Gliders cannot be tamed or ridden. They are passive and live in areas above -105°C. They enjoy music and can be eaten.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.24.text",
          "label": "Extraterrestrial Fauna",
          "item_ref_count": 27,
          "count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna.json",
          "text": "Crushers are semihostile animals found in herds in areas above -100°C. They can be killed for their meat and hard spikes.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.25.text",
          "label": "Extraterrestrial Fauna",
          "item_ref_count": 27,
          "count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna.json",
          "text": "Springlings are wild animals found above -108°C. Feeding them Saplings will let you ride them and extend their extremely long necks. They can also be eaten.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.26.text",
          "label": "Extraterrestrial Fauna",
          "item_ref_count": 27,
          "count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna.json",
          "text": "Goobers are lazy wild animals found above -109°C. They are passive and can be eaten.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.27.text",
          "label": "Extraterrestrial Fauna",
          "item_ref_count": 27,
          "count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna.json",
          "text": "The gigantic Walkers inhabit areas above -97°C. They are passive and can be eaten.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.28.text",
          "label": "Extraterrestrial Fauna",
          "item_ref_count": 27,
          "count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna.json",
          "text": "Raiders are hostile animals found above -109°C. They have a natural rivalry with Chargers. You can make them neutral by feeding them Raw Meat.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.29.text",
          "label": "Extraterrestrial Fauna",
          "item_ref_count": 27,
          "count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna.json",
          "text": "Toxlacanths are aquatic prey animals. It is recommended to kill them from a distance and cook their meat before eating.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.30.text",
          "label": "Extraterrestrial Fauna",
          "item_ref_count": 27,
          "count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/space_fauna.json",
          "text": "One of the few animals that can survive the Martian desert, Stackaticks are passive prey animals. They can also be eaten.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.31.text",
          "label": "Extraterrestrial Fauna",
          "item_ref_count": 27,
          "count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "advancement",
          "id": "species:species/v2/ancient_eggs",
          "source": "jar:species-3.5.jar!data/species/advancements/species/v2/ancient_eggs.json",
          "text": "We're Back",
          "key": "advancement-title:title",
          "label": "We're Back",
          "item_ref_count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "advancement",
          "id": "species:species/v2/ancient_eggs",
          "source": "jar:species-3.5.jar!data/species/advancements/species/v2/ancient_eggs.json",
          "text": "Have all Ancient Eggs in your inventory",
          "key": "advancement-description:description",
          "label": "We're Back",
          "item_ref_count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "First, like the Moon, Mars will have some hostile surface mobs as well. If you haven't already figured out how to make your Space Suit stay with you after dying or invested in new EV-tier weaponry, you'll have a rough time.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "The planet is mostly a desert world, dotted with \"islands\" where you’ll find water, lush fauna, and much safer places to build your first base. Meanwhile, the open desert is extremely dangerous if you don't watch your step, but contains some other crucial resources!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "{@pagebreak}",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "All fluid veins are spread across Mars, except for Heavy Ammoniacial Water which can only be found outside of the desert. That’s another good reason to set up your base there.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Energy-wise, Mars will not be kind to you at first.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Nearly every common energy generation method is disabled or worthless on the planet, which means you’ll have to rely almost entirely on Fission to power your base.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "At the very beginning, we recommend bringing along some filled batteries such as Lapotron Crystals to get started, and then look towards setting up Thorium Fuel Rods.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Achieving energy stability on Mars will take time and effort, but once your systems are running smoothly, you’ll feel like unlimited power is right in the palm of your hand.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "And one last tip: don’t kill everything you see. Some animals can be ranched, and you’ll definitely need them later in your progression.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Be sure to check out the Space Survival chapter for more Mars-related tips too!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Stay alert, plan ahead, and Mars will reward your courage.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "The Red Planet",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Land on Mars",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "I'm on Mars!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "one_completed",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "The water on Mars is Semiheavy which means it contains one Deuterium atom, and is also full of Ammonia, a great antifreeze, which is how it can stay liquid despite the very low temperatures.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "It's technically drinkable, but you'll likely want to distill it into normal Water first. You can also move source blocks of Semiheavy Ammoniacal Water around and get more of it via an Aqueous Accumulator, just like regular Water.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "You can also centrifuge the Ammonium Chloride out from it and electrolyze it as a source of Nitrogen. Easy Nitrox from just one source!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Fancy a drink?",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Semiheavy Ammoniacal Water",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Dotted around the vast Martian deserts are small \"islands\" of life and vegetation, which are probably where you'll want to set up your base. Surviving here will be much easier than in the desert!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Is that a dinosaur?!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        }
      ],
      "aliases": []
    },
    {

      "semantic_evidence": [
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking_applications",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking_applications.json",
          "text": "Glass Products",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Glass Products",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking_applications",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking_applications.json",
          "text": "The most simple glass products are Glass Panes and Glass Blocks. In order to craft them, you must start with a Blowpipe with a Glass Batch, and then perform a Pour. - Table Pours are used to create Glass Panes - Basin Pours are used to create Glass Blocks",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Glass Products",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking_applications",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking_applications.json",
          "text": "Glass can also be dyed before it is poured to create colored glass. The color is dependent on the type of glass batch, and any powders that have been added. Each type of Glass Batch has a different natural color of glass that they will create. Silica glass batches can be made into many colors, Olivine, and Volcanic glass can be made into relatively few colors.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Glass Products",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking_applications",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking_applications.json",
          "text": "Glass Panes are made with a Table Pour. A pouring table is made by placing up to sixteen Kaolin Clay Blocks or Brass Plated Blocks in a continuous area. 1. Add a Glass Batch to a Blowpipe. 2. Heat the blowpipe to Faint Red. 3. the Blowpipe on the top of the table. 4. Finally with a Paddle to flatten the glass.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Glass Products",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking_applications",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking_applications.json",
          "text": "Table Pour",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.title",
          "label": "Glass Products",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking_applications",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking_applications.json",
          "text": "Once the glass is cooled, it can be broken with a Gem Saw to obtain.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Glass Products",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking_applications",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking_applications.json",
          "text": "Glass Blocks are made with a Basin Pour. A basin is made by surrounding all sides of an air block except the top with Kaolin Clay Blocks or Brass Plated Blocks. 1. Add a Glass Batch to a Blowpipe. 2. Heat the blowpipe to Faint Red. 3. the Blowpipe on the top of the table.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Glass Products",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking_applications",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking_applications.json",
          "text": "Basin Pour",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.title",
          "label": "Glass Products",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking_applications",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking_applications.json",
          "text": "Once the glass is cooled, it can be broken with a Gem Saw to obtain.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Glass Products",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking_applications",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking_applications.json",
          "text": "Glassworking Blocks",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.6.title",
          "label": "Glass Products",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking_applications",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking_applications.json",
          "text": "Brass plated blocks can be created by holding a brass plate and on any stone brick.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.6.text",
          "label": "Glass Products",
          "item_ref_count": 8,

        {
          "kind": "guide_page",

          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking_applications.json",
          "text": "Glass has a natural color based on the type of Glass Batch that was used. Other colors can be made using a Bowl. To use, place the Bowl on the ground, then the required Powder. Before Pouring, use the Blowpipe on the bowl to add the powder to the batch.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.7.text",
          "label": "Glass Products",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 16
        },

          "text": "Coloring Glass",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.7.title",
          "label": "Glass Products",

        {

          "text": "- White: Silica or Hematitic Glass + Soda Ash - Black: Any Glass + Graphite - Gray: Any + Graphite + Soda Ash - Light Gray: Any + Graphite + 2x Soda Ash - Purple: Any + Iron + Copper - Brown: Any + Nickel - Cyan: Non-Volcanic Glass + Copper + Sapphire - Green: Silica or Hematitic Glass + Iron",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.8.text",
          "label": "Glass Products",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 16
        },

          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking_applications.json",

          "label": "Glass Products",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",

          "text": "- Lime: Silica or Hematitic Glass + Iron + Soda Ash - Light Blue: Silica Glass + Lapis Lazuli - Blue: Silica Glass + Copper - Red: Silica or Hematitic Glass + Tin - Yellow: Silica or Hematitic Glass + Silver - Orange: Silica Glass + Pyrite - Magenta: Silica or Hematitic Glass + Ruby - Pink: Silica Glass + Gold - Tinted: Non-Silica Glass + Amethyst",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.9.text",
          "label": "Glass Products",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 16
        },
        {

          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking_applications",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking_applications.json",

        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking_applications",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking_applications.json",
          "text": "Lamp Glass is a necessary component to craft Lamps.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.12.text",
          "label": "Glass Products",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking_applications",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking_applications.json",
          "text": "Jars are also made from blown glass, but only silica or hematitic glass.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.13.text",
          "label": "Glass Products",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking_applications",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking_applications.json",
          "text": "Glass Bottles can also be made. The quality of the glass bottle depends on the type of glass used to make it.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.14.text",
          "label": "Glass Products",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 16
        },

          "text": "The lens is used for crafting the spyglass, compass, and other things.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.15.text",
          "label": "Glass Products",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "advancement",
          "id": "tfc:story/basin_pour",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!data/tfc/advancements/story/basin_pour.json",
          "text": "Basin Pour",
          "key": "advancement-title:title",
          "label": "Basin Pour",
          "item_ref_count": 1
        },
        {
          "kind": "advancement",
          "id": "tfc:story/basin_pour",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!data/tfc/advancements/story/basin_pour.json",
          "text": "Pour some glass into a basin",
          "key": "advancement-description:description",
          "label": "Basin Pour",
          "item_ref_count": 1
        },
        {
          "kind": "advancement",
          "id": "tfc:story/glass_colors",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!data/tfc/advancements/story/glass_colors.json",
          "text": "Stained Glass",
          "key": "advancement-title:title",

          "item_ref_count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "advancement",
          "id": "tfc:story/glass_colors",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!data/tfc/advancements/story/glass_colors.json",
          "text": "Make all colors of stained glass blocks, as well as clear and tinted glass",
          "key": "advancement-description:description",
          "label": "Stained Glass",
          "item_ref_count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking_applications",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking_applications.json",
          "text": "Glass Products",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Glass Products",
          "item_ref_count": 13,
          "recipe_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking_applications",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking_applications.json",
          "text": "The most simple glass products are Glass Panes and Glass Blocks. In order to craft them, you must start with a Blowpipe with a Glass Batch, and then perform a Pour. - Table Pours are used to create Glass Panes - Basin Pours are used to create Glass Blocks",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Glass Products",
          "item_ref_count": 13,
          "recipe_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking_applications",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking_applications.json",
          "text": "Glass can also be dyed before it is poured to create colored glass. The color is dependent on the type of glass batch, and any powders that have been added. Each type of Glass Batch has a different natural color of glass that they will create. Silica glass batches can be made into many colors, Olivine, and Volcanic glass can be made into relatively few colors.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Glass Products",
          "item_ref_count": 13,
          "recipe_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking_applications",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking_applications.json",
          "text": "Glass Panes are made with a Table Pour. A pouring table is made by placing up to sixteen Brass Plated Blocks in a continuous area. 1. Add a Glass Batch to a Blowpipe. 2. Heat the blowpipe to Faint Red. 3. the Blowpipe on the top of the table. 4. Finally with a Paddle to flatten the glass.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Glass Products",
          "item_ref_count": 13,
          "recipe_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking_applications",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking_applications.json",
          "text": "Table Pour",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.title",
          "label": "Glass Products",
          "item_ref_count": 13,
          "recipe_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking_applications",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking_applications.json",
          "text": "Once the glass is cooled, it can be broken with a Gem Saw to obtain.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Glass Products",
          "item_ref_count": 13,
          "recipe_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking_applications",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking_applications.json",
          "text": "Glass Blocks are made with a Basin Pour. A basin is made by surrounding all sides of an air block except the top with Brass Plated Blocks. 1. Add a Glass Batch to a Blowpipe. 2. Heat the blowpipe to Faint Red. 3. the Blowpipe on the top of the table.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Glass Products",
          "item_ref_count": 13,
          "recipe_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking_applications",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking_applications.json",
          "text": "Basin Pour",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.title",
          "label": "Glass Products",
          "item_ref_count": 13,
          "recipe_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking_applications",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking_applications.json",
          "text": "Once the glass is cooled, it can be broken with a Gem Saw to obtain.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Glass Products",
          "item_ref_count": 13,
          "recipe_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 16
        },

          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking_applications.json",
          "text": "Glass has a natural color based on the type of Glass Batch that was used. Other colors can be made using a Bowl. To use, place the Bowl on the ground, then the required Powder. Before Pouring, use the Blowpipe on the bowl to add the powder to the batch.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.6.text",
          "label": "Glass Products",

        },

          "text": "Coloring Glass",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.6.title",

          "item_ref_count": 13,
          "recipe_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 16
        },

          "text": "Glass Bottles can also be made. The quality of the glass bottle depends on the type of glass used to make it.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.14.text",
          "label": "Glass Products",
          "item_ref_count": 13,
          "recipe_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking_applications",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking_applications.json",
          "text": "The Lens is used for crafting the spyglass, compasses, and daylight sensors.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.15.text",
          "label": "Glass Products",
          "item_ref_count": 13,
          "recipe_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "runtime_item",
          "id": "minecraft:glass",
          "source": "runtime-items",
          "text": "(SiO₂)",
          "key": "runtime-tooltip",
          "label": "Glass",
          "item_ref_count": 1
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel.json",
          "text": "Better Fuel",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Better Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel.json",
          "text": "Cryo Freezer",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.title",
          "label": "Better Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel.json",
          "text": "Fuel is hard to get; it requires venturing out to the ocean, looking for oil spouts. The Cryo Freezer introduces a new way to obtain a better fuel - Cryo Fuel. Cryo Fuel is much more efficient than refined fuel, allowing you to launch a rocket with just one bucket.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Better Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel.json",
          "text": "It's created by collecting ice shards, which are found as an ore underground on cold planets. The ice shard then needs to be inserted into a Cryo Freezer, and it will convert to 25 mB of Cryo Fuel. Alternatively, you can convert ice, packed ice and blue ice to fuel. Once you've made at least two buckets (one for a launch and the other for a return trip), place the buckets in the rocket fuel slot.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Better Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel.json",
          "text": "Cryo Fuel",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.title",
          "label": "Better Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel.json",
          "text": "In addition to being an excellent fuel, cryo fuel has some other features. When placed, it will damage anything inside, similar to lava. It also freezes water, allowing for an infinite ice source.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Better Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/detection",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/detection.json",
          "text": "Oxygen Sensor",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.title",
          "label": "Detection",
          "item_ref_count": 3,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/detection",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/detection.json",
          "text": "The oxygen sensor is a block that will emit a redstone signal when it detects oxygen. You can shift-right-click it to invert the signal. Despite its name, you can also change its detection type by right-clicking with a wrench. It has the option to detect oxygen, safe temperature, and normal gravity.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Detection",
          "item_ref_count": 3,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/energizer",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/energizer.json",
          "text": "The energizer is a machine capable of powering items and storing large amounts of energy. To charge an item, power the energizer and then right-click on the energizer with that item. It stores 2 million energy, making it capable of charging machines for a very long time. It also retains its energy when broken.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Energizer",
          "item_ref_count": 3,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/flags",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/flags.json",
          "text": "Flags are decorative blocks available in 16 colors. By default, when placed, they will display the face of the flag owner. They can be configured to display a custom image from a URL by right-clicking and entering the URL in the GUI. This will display the internet image onto the flag.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Flags",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 2
        }
      ],
      "aliases": []
    },

        {

          "source": "file:minecraft/lang-overlays",
          "text": "Select a Train Track then place the Station nearby",

          "label": "Assembling Trains",
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "create:ponder/train_assembly",
          "source": "file:minecraft/lang-overlays",
          "text": "Every Train requires Train Controls on board",
          "key": "lang-ponder:create.ponder.train_assembly.text_10",
          "label": "Assembling Trains",
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "create:ponder/train_assembly",
          "source": "file:minecraft/lang-overlays",
          "text": "An optional second one allows departure from Stations in both directions",
          "key": "lang-ponder:create.ponder.train_assembly.text_11",
          "label": "Assembling Trains",
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "create:ponder/train_assembly",
          "source": "file:minecraft/lang-overlays",
          "text": "Open the Station UI and confirm the Assembly process",
          "key": "lang-ponder:create.ponder.train_assembly.text_12",
          "label": "Assembling Trains",
          "count": 16
        },
        {

          "id": "create:ponder/train_assembly",
          "source": "file:minecraft/lang-overlays",
          "text": "Trains can be disassembled back into blocks at stations only",

          "label": "Assembling Trains",
          "count": 16
        },
        {

          "source": "file:minecraft/lang-overlays",
          "text": "When used on a station, maps will add a labeled marker at the location",

          "source": "file:minecraft/lang-overlays",

          "label": "Assembling Trains",
          "count": 16

          "id": "create:ponder/train_assembly",
          "source": "file:minecraft/lang-overlays",
          "text": "Stations are the Waypoints of your Track Network",
          "key": "lang-ponder:create.ponder.train_assembly.text_2",
          "label": "Assembling Trains",
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "create:ponder/train_assembly",
          "source": "file:minecraft/lang-overlays",
          "text": "To create a new Train, open the UI and switch to Assembly Mode",
          "key": "lang-ponder:create.ponder.train_assembly.text_3",
          "label": "Assembling Trains",
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "create:ponder/train_assembly",
          "source": "file:minecraft/lang-overlays",
          "text": "During Assembly no scheduled trains will approach this station",

          "label": "Assembling Trains",

        },

          "source": "file:minecraft/lang-overlays",
          "text": "Create new bogeys by using Train Casing on Tracks",

          "source": "file:minecraft/lang-overlays",

          "label": "Assembling Trains",

      "id": "create:using/the/deployer",
      "label": "Using the Deployer",
      "origin": "namespace_generated",
      "confidence": 0.7,
      "support": 32,
      "evidence": [
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "create:ponder/deployer",
          "confidence": 0.7
        }
      ],
      "semantic_evidence": [
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "create:ponder/deployer",
          "source": "file:minecraft/lang-overlays",
          "text": "Using the Deployer",
          "key": "lang-ponder:create.ponder.deployer.header",
          "label": "Using the Deployer",
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "create:ponder/deployer",
          "source": "file:minecraft/lang-overlays",
          "text": "Given Rotational Force, a Deployer can imitate player interactions",
          "key": "lang-ponder:create.ponder.deployer.text_1",
          "label": "Using the Deployer",
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "create:ponder/deployer",
          "source": "file:minecraft/lang-overlays",
          "text": "Right-click the front to give it an Item to use",
          "key": "lang-ponder:create.ponder.deployer.text_10",
          "label": "Using the Deployer",
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "create:ponder/deployer",
          "source": "file:minecraft/lang-overlays",
          "text": "Items can also be inserted automatically",
          "key": "lang-ponder:create.ponder.deployer.text_11",
          "label": "Using the Deployer",
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "create:ponder/deployer",
          "source": "file:minecraft/lang-overlays",
          "text": "Deployers carry a filter slot",
          "key": "lang-ponder:create.ponder.deployer.text_12",
          "label": "Using the Deployer",
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "create:ponder/deployer",
          "source": "file:minecraft/lang-overlays",
          "text": "When a filter is set, it activates only while holding a matching item",
          "key": "lang-ponder:create.ponder.deployer.text_13",
          "label": "Using the Deployer",
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "create:ponder/deployer",
          "source": "file:minecraft/lang-overlays",
          "text": "Only items matching the filter can now be inserted...",
          "key": "lang-ponder:create.ponder.deployer.text_14",
          "label": "Using the Deployer",
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "create:ponder/deployer",
          "source": "file:minecraft/lang-overlays",
          "text": "...and only non-matching items will be extracted",
          "key": "lang-ponder:create.ponder.deployer.text_15",
          "label": "Using the Deployer",
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "create:ponder/deployer",
          "source": "file:minecraft/lang-overlays",
          "text": "It will always interact with the position 2 blocks in front of itself",
          "key": "lang-ponder:create.ponder.deployer.text_2",
          "label": "Using the Deployer",
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "create:ponder/deployer",
          "source": "file:minecraft/lang-overlays",
          "text": "Blocks directly in front will not obstruct it",
          "key": "lang-ponder:create.ponder.deployer.text_3",
          "label": "Using the Deployer",
          "count": 16
        },
        {

    {
      "id": "tfc:anvil/working/guide",
      "label": "Anvil Working Guide",
      "origin": "namespace_generated",
      "confidence": 0.7,
      "support": 30,
      "evidence": [
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide",
