        }
      ],
      "semantic_evidence": [
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide.json",
          "text": "Anvil Working Guide",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Anvil Working Guide",
          "item_ref_count": 17,
          "count": 30
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide.json",
          "text": "Working with Anvils",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.title",
          "label": "Anvil Working Guide",
          "item_ref_count": 17,
          "count": 30
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide.json",
          "text": "Working in Anvils is a key process inside TerraFirmaGreg, as most of your work towards the Steam and Mechanical ages will require the usage of an Anvil. This guide is broken down into two parts, Understanding the UI and How to Perfectly Forge. The Modpack has a custom Resource Pack called TFC Anvil Helper that you need to enable for this guide to make sense.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Anvil Working Guide",
          "item_ref_count": 17,
          "count": 30
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide.json",
          "text": "The UI for the Anvil with TFC Anvil Helper Enabled",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Anvil Working Guide",
          "item_ref_count": 17,
          "count": 30
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide.json",
          "text": "The UI for the Anvil with TFC Anvil Helper Enabled, Color Coded.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Anvil Working Guide",
          "item_ref_count": 17,
          "count": 30
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide.json",
          "text": "1. The item you've selected to Create in the Anvil. Each item has a specific set of required Steps and Work Amount 2. The Input item you're currently working. 3. The required Steps in Order that must be completed before the Item is considered complete. There are a total of 4 different Orders (See next Page):",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Anvil Working Guide",
          "item_ref_count": 17,
          "count": 30
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide.json",
          "text": "(Note: X is a wild-card for any kind of Step) - x_Any: Step can occurr in any of the last 3 actions. - x_Last: Must be the last step performed. - x_Not Last: The Step cannot be the Last Step performed. - x_Second Last: Must be the Second to Last step performed. - x_Third Last: Must be the Third to Last step performed",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Anvil Working Guide",
          "item_ref_count": 17,
          "count": 30
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide.json",
          "text": "4. The types of Steps. Each type of step Subtracts or Adds to the current Work value. These are, in order from Left to Right, Top to Bottom: - Light Hit: -3 - Medium Hit: -6 - Punch: +2 - Bend: +7 - Hard Hit: -9 - Draw: -15 - Upset: +13 - Shrink: +16",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.7.text",
          "label": "Anvil Working Guide",
          "item_ref_count": 17,
          "count": 30
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide.json",
          "text": "5. The Slots where your Hammer goes, and Flux if youre Welding. 6. The current Work amount the item has, Doing different Steps either Increases or Decreases the amount of Work the item has. 7. The Target Work value, the Work value must be aligned EXACTLY with the Target Work, AND the Required Steps must be fulfilled for the Item to be considered complete. 8. The Work Bar, used for Aligning the Work value with the Target Work value.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.9.text",
          "label": "Anvil Working Guide",
          "item_ref_count": 17,
          "count": 30
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide.json",
          "text": "How to Perfectly Forge",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.10.title",
          "label": "Anvil Working Guide",
          "item_ref_count": 17,
          "count": 30
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide.json",
          "text": "Perfectly Forging an Item which can break, such as Tool Heads, Weapons, and Armor causes the Item to gain bonuses to it's durability and general efficiency with the less total steps you take to forge the Item. Completing the Work on an Item with very few steps causes it to be Perfectly Forged, not only giving your finished item bonuses, but also reducing the overall wear and tear to the Hammer.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.10.text",
          "label": "Anvil Working Guide",
          "item_ref_count": 17,
          "count": 30
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide.json",
          "text": "A Perfectly Forged Wrought Iron Pickaxe Head",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.11.text",
          "label": "Anvil Working Guide",
          "item_ref_count": 17,
          "count": 30
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide.json",
          "text": "Guide to Forging",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.12.title",
          "label": "Anvil Working Guide",
          "item_ref_count": 17,
          "count": 30
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide.json",
          "text": "This part of the guide will cover how to Perfectly Forge anything, starting with a Wrought Iron Pickaxe Head. DISCLAIMER: While the Required Steps and Orders for an Item are universal within all worlds in TerraFirmaGreg, the Target Work value differs because its derived from your world's Seed, as such, this Guide ASSUMES the Target Work value for the Wrought Iron Pickaxe Head is 83",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.12.text",
          "label": "Anvil Working Guide",
          "item_ref_count": 17,
          "count": 30
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide.json",
          "text": "Step 1",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.14.title",
          "label": "Anvil Working Guide",
          "item_ref_count": 17,
          "count": 30
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide.json",
          "text": "The first Step in your Forging Journey is to get the Value taken by Adding all of the Required Step's values together. For a Pickaxe Head, you need the following Steps: - Punch Last - Bend Not Last - Draw Not Last. By adding the Values on the Bottom of each Step, We get the Value we're looking for. Lets call this value \"requiredWork\". 2 + 7 + (-15) = -6",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.14.text",
          "label": "Anvil Working Guide",
          "item_ref_count": 17,
          "count": 30
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide.json",
          "text": "The steps required for a Pickaxe",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.15.text",
          "label": "Anvil Working Guide",
          "item_ref_count": 17,
          "count": 30
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide.json",
          "text": "Step 2",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.16.title",
          "label": "Anvil Working Guide",
          "item_ref_count": 17,
          "count": 30
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide.json",
          "text": "While our main goal for the Work Amount is to align it with the Target Work, we need to make sure both that requirement AND the Steps requirements are fulfilled, as such, directly aligning the Work Amount with the Target Work will not yield desirable results. Instead, we can calculate the a value derived from Target Work and \"requiredWork\"",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.16.text",
          "label": "Anvil Working Guide",
          "item_ref_count": 17,
          "count": 30
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide.json",
          "text": "This value can be obtained depending on the Sign of \"requiredWork\" (IE: if the value is Negative or Positive). - If \"requiredWork\" is PositiveSubtract \"requiredWork\"'s Absolute Value from \"Target Work\"83 - 6 == 77 - If \"requiredWork\" is NegativeAdd \"requiredWork\"'s Absolute Value to \"Target Work\"83 + 6 = 89",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.17.text",
          "label": "Anvil Working Guide",
          "item_ref_count": 17,
          "count": 30
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide.json",
          "text": "Since the \"requiredWork\" for our item is -6, the value we're looking for is 89. You may be wondering, how can you get exact numerical values for your Target Work? Well, the Anvil UI shows us with the Work Bar. It only shows the multiples of 20, but we can know the other numbers easily via the colored lines: - Light Gray: Represents a multiple of 5 - Gray: Represents an Odd Number - Dark Gray: Represents an Even Number",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.18.text",
          "label": "Anvil Working Guide",
          "item_ref_count": 17,
          "count": 30
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide.json",
          "text": "Step 3",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.20.title",
          "label": "Anvil Working Guide",
          "item_ref_count": 17,
          "count": 30
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide.json",
          "text": "With the value derived from \"Target Work\" and \"requiredWork\", we now need to reach said Work value, this can be done either manually by adding the numbers attached for each step, or by using the cheat sheet in the UI bar. Each color represents the Step Type in the UI. IE: Red == Shrink (+16)",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.20.text",
          "label": "Anvil Working Guide",
          "item_ref_count": 17,
          "count": 30
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide.json",
          "text": "16 + 16 + 16 + 16 + 16 + 7 + 2 = 89",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.21.text",
          "label": "Anvil Working Guide",
          "item_ref_count": 17,
          "count": 30
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide.json",
          "text": "Step 4",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.22.title",
          "label": "Anvil Working Guide",
          "item_ref_count": 17,
          "count": 30
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide.json",
          "text": "Now that we know the required steps, its time to reach the Value derived from \"Target Work\" and \"requiredWork\". For a pickaxe head where it's TotalWork value is 83, we need to reach 89. As such, we do: (Shrink x 5) -> Bend -> Punch. Once our Work value is at our desired value, just follow the Required Steps, which for a pickaxe head its: Draw -> Bend -> Punch. And Voila! We've obtained a Perfectly Forged Item!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.22.text",
          "label": "Anvil Working Guide",
          "item_ref_count": 17,
          "count": 30
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide.json",
          "text": "An Ingot with No Work->Desired Work Value Reached->Doing the Required Steps for our Item.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.23.text",
          "label": "Anvil Working Guide",
          "item_ref_count": 17,
          "count": 30
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide.json",
          "text": "Lets see this process with other items, such as a Plate, or a Rod. Again, the Target Work differs by the world seed, so their values will be different on your world. Plate Target Value: 111 Required Steps: Hit x 3, Any Order. \"requiredWork\": -3 x 3 = -9 Derived Value: 111 + 9 = 120. Reaching the Value: (Shrink x 5)->(Upset x 2)->(Punch x 2) Getting the Item: 120 - (Light Hit x 3) == 111. Both requirements met.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.24.text",
          "label": "Anvil Working Guide",
          "item_ref_count": 17,
          "count": 30
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide.json",
          "text": "Rod Target Value: 78 Required Steps: Draw->Draw->Bend \"requiredWork\": (-15 x 2) + 7 = -23 Derived Value: 78 + 23 = 101. Reaching the Value: (Shrink x 5)->(Bend x 3) Getting the Item: 101 - 15 - 15 + 7 = 78.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.25.text",
          "label": "Anvil Working Guide",
          "item_ref_count": 17,
          "count": 30
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide.json",
          "text": "Other Tidbits",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.26.title",
          "label": "Anvil Working Guide",
          "item_ref_count": 17,
          "count": 30
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide.json",
          "text": "If one of the Required Steps is Empty, it means it's a Wild Card, any Step can be used. If you fail your calculations, you can always melt and cast your item back into an ingot, this will remove the \"worked\" tag for the item, letting you try again to perfectly forge it.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.26.text",
          "label": "Anvil Working Guide",
          "item_ref_count": 17,
          "count": 30
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide.json",
          "text": "Congratulations on Reaching the End, if youre a contributor looking to re-take the pictures in this guide, the seed used during it's writing is -7291708953208913102, You could also create a creative world to test the algorithm yourself! Also... since you're here, if you cant be bothered to do all the calculations yourself, you can always use the TFC Anvil Calculator...",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.29.text",
          "label": "Anvil Working Guide",
          "item_ref_count": 17,
          "count": 30
        },
        {

        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/beneath_wild_fruits",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/beneath_wild_fruits.json",
          "text": "Beneath Wild Fruits",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Beneath Wild Fruits",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 8
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/beneath_wild_fruits",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/beneath_wild_fruits.json",
          "text": "The Beneath hosts its own unique selection of wild fruits, which have adapted to the extreme conditions underground. Wild fruits can be cultivated in all the same ways as regular wild fruits in the Overworld.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Beneath Wild Fruits",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 8
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/beneath_wild_fruits",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/beneath_wild_fruits.json",
          "text": "- Lavacado - Magmango",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Beneath Wild Fruits",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 8
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/beneath_wild_fruits",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/beneath_wild_fruits.json",
          "text": "Temperature: 8 - 80 °C Hydration: 20 - 100 % Lavacado trees grow in the months of December through April, start flowering in May and June, and bear fruit in July.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Beneath Wild Fruits",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 8
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/beneath_wild_fruits",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/beneath_wild_fruits.json",
          "text": "An example lavacado tree.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Beneath Wild Fruits",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 8
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/beneath_wild_fruits",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/beneath_wild_fruits.json",
          "text": "Temperature: 8 - 80 °C Hydration: 30 - 100 % Magmango trees grow in the months of October through February, start flowering in March and April, and bear fruit in May.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Beneath Wild Fruits",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 8
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/beneath_wild_fruits",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/beneath_wild_fruits.json",
          "text": "An example magmango tree.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Beneath Wild Fruits",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 8
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/beneath_wild_fruits",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/beneath_wild_fruits.json",
          "text": "The fruiting calendar for fruit trees.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.6.text",
          "label": "Beneath Wild Fruits",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 8
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",

          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/beneath_wild_fruits.json",
          "text": "Fruit Trees",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.6.title",
          "label": "Beneath Wild Fruits",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 8
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/arborfirmacraft",
          "source": "jar:firmacivplus-1.0.1-1.20.1.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/arborfirmacraft.json",
          "text": "If you see Tualang or Fig trees in your travels, you'll be pleased to know that you'll be able to create Boats with them just like any other kind of Wood",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "ArborFirmaCraft",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/arborfirmacraft",
          "source": "jar:firmacivplus-1.0.1-1.20.1.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/arborfirmacraft.json",
          "text": "Softwoods have sufficiently soft enough pulp and bark that they're perfect for creating Dugout Canoes",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "ArborFirmaCraft",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/arborfirmacraft",
          "source": "jar:firmacivplus-1.0.1-1.20.1.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/arborfirmacraft.json",
          "text": "- Tualang - Fig - Cypress",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "ArborFirmaCraft",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/arborfirmacraft",
          "source": "jar:firmacivplus-1.0.1-1.20.1.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/arborfirmacraft.json",
          "text": "Known for their exceptional sturdiness, Hardwood trees are ideal for creating larger and sophisticated Watercrafts such as Rowboats and Sloops",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "ArborFirmaCraft",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/arborfirmacraft",
          "source": "jar:firmacivplus-1.0.1-1.20.1.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/arborfirmacraft.json",
          "text": "- Baobab - Eucalyptus - Mahogany - Hevea - Teak - Ironwood - Ipe",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "ArborFirmaCraft",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/beneath",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/beneath.json",
          "text": "Martian Boats",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Martian Boats",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/beneath",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/beneath.json",
          "text": "Way off on the far reaches of Mars, you can find both Crimson and Warped wood to create some rather flashy Boats.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Martian Boats",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/beneath",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/beneath.json",
          "text": "Softwoods have sufficiently soft enough pulp and bark that they're perfect for creating Dugout Canoes",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Martian Boats",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/beneath",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/beneath.json",
          "text": "- Crimson",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Martian Boats",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/beneath",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/beneath.json",
          "text": "Known for their exceptional sturdiness, Hardwood trees are ideal for creating larger and sophisticated Watercrafts such as Rowboats and Sloops",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Martian Boats",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/beneath",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/beneath.json",
          "text": "- Warped",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Martian Boats",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/beneath",
          "source": "jar:firmacivplus-1.0.1-1.20.1.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/beneath.json",
          "text": "Deep within the nether you can utilize both Crimson and Warped wood to create some rather flashy Boats. Format Error: Due to a limitation, Watercrafts created with these materials are NOT impervious to lava, you WILL sink and BURN.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Beneath",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 6
        }

    {
      "id": "tfc:hazardous/materials",
      "label": "Hazardous Materials",
      "origin": "namespace_generated",
      "confidence": 0.7,
      "support": 29,
      "evidence": [
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_ores/hazards",
          "confidence": 0.7
        }
      ],
      "semantic_evidence": [
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_ores/hazards",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_ores/hazards.json",
          "text": "Hazardous Materials",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Hazardous Materials",
          "item_ref_count": 28,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 29
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_ores/hazards",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_ores/hazards.json",
          "text": "Hazardous Materials",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.title",
          "label": "Hazardous Materials",
          "item_ref_count": 28,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 29
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_ores/hazards",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_ores/hazards.json",
          "text": "Some ores, dusts and chemicals are hazardous and can cause negative effects or even kill you if they are carried in your inventory. When carried, they are displayed on screen with a Status Effect. Certain equipment such as masks, gloves, and the hazardous material suit can be used to counter these effects.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Hazardous Materials",
          "item_ref_count": 28,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 29
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_ores/hazards",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_ores/hazards.json",
          "text": "All of the effects",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.title",
          "label": "Hazardous Materials",
          "item_ref_count": 28,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 29
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_ores/hazards",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_ores/hazards.json",
          "text": "- Arsenicosis - Asbestosis - Berylliosis - Carbon Monoxide poisoning - Carcinogens - Chemical burns - Irritants - Methanol poisoning - Nauseating chemicals - Poisonous materials - Radioactive materials - Silicosis The next page will provide information on how to deal with these effects.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Hazardous Materials",
          "item_ref_count": 28,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 29
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_ores/hazards",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_ores/hazards.json",
          "text": "Prevention and Cures",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.title",
          "label": "Hazardous Materials",
          "item_ref_count": 28,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 29
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_ores/hazards",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_ores/hazards.json",
          "text": "- Rocks? - Containers - The Face Mask - The Rubber Gloves - The Hazmat Suit - Pills, Tablets and Salvos - The RadAwayᵗᵐ pill - The Unthinkable...",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Hazardous Materials",
          "item_ref_count": 28,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 29
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_ores/hazards",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_ores/hazards.json",
          "text": "Rocks!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.title",
          "label": "Hazardous Materials",
          "item_ref_count": 28,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 29
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_ores/hazards",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_ores/hazards.json",
          "text": "The easiest way to prevent hazardous effects is to not pick up the dangerous items at all. Filling your inventory with Rocks, for example, will let you pick and choose which items you want to pick up, letting you deal with the hazardous ones later.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Hazardous Materials",
          "item_ref_count": 28,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 29
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_ores/hazards",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_ores/hazards.json",
          "text": "Hazardous materials only deal damage while they are in your inventory, but that doesn't apply to other containers! Hazardous materials will not deal damage while they are in another container such as a Backpack or Vessel. Right-clicking while holding a vessel will allow direct transfer of its contents to and from containers.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Hazardous Materials",
          "item_ref_count": 28,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 29
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_ores/hazards",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_ores/hazards.json",
          "text": "Face Mask",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.title",
          "label": "Hazardous Materials",
          "item_ref_count": 28,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 29
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_ores/hazards",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_ores/hazards.json",
          "text": "The Face Mask provides protection from any Inhalation hazards, such as Arsenicosis.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Hazardous Materials",
          "item_ref_count": 28,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 29
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_ores/hazards",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_ores/hazards.json",
          "text": "Rubber gloves",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.6.title",
          "label": "Hazardous Materials",
          "item_ref_count": 28,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 29
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_ores/hazards",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_ores/hazards.json",
          "text": "The Rubber gloves can either be made from Leather and Latex, or out of Rubber Sheets. They provide protection from any Skin Contact hazards, such as Beryllium Dust.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.6.text",
          "label": "Hazardous Materials",
          "item_ref_count": 28,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 29
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_ores/hazards",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_ores/hazards.json",
          "text": "First, knap the leather into mittens.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.7.text",
          "label": "Hazardous Materials",
          "item_ref_count": 28,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 29
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_ores/hazards",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_ores/hazards.json",
          "text": "Mittens are then soaked in Vinegar, a mixture of fruit and alcohol.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.8.text",
          "label": "Hazardous Materials",
          "item_ref_count": 28,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 29
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_ores/hazards",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_ores/hazards.json",
          "text": "Prepared leather gloves are then heated in a vat with Vulcanized Latex, one step away from rubber.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.9.text",
          "label": "Hazardous Materials",
          "item_ref_count": 28,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 29
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_ores/hazards",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_ores/hazards.json",
          "text": "Finally, the latex-soaked gloves are heated in an Oven.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.10.text",
          "label": "Hazardous Materials",
          "item_ref_count": 28,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 29
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_ores/hazards",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_ores/hazards.json",
          "text": "The Hazmat Suit",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.11.title",
          "label": "Hazardous Materials",
          "item_ref_count": 28,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 29
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_ores/hazards",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_ores/hazards.json",
          "text": "A completed Hazmat suit provides immunity from every hazard, even radiation. It is made in an LV Assembler from Polyethylene and Polyvinyl Chloride. Fun fact! You can also look up #ppe_armor in JEI for more equipment that provides protection from hazardous materials.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.11.text",
          "label": "Hazardous Materials",
          "item_ref_count": 28,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 29
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_ores/hazards",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_ores/hazards.json",
          "text": "In case you do get heavily exposed to a hazard, there are some Medicines that can help you recover. Basic Pills are the most primitive and easy to make, but most come with Negative Side Effects. Tablets and Salvos are more effective and have less side effects, but they require LV equipment and more advanced resources to make.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.12.text",
          "label": "Hazardous Materials",
          "item_ref_count": 28,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 29
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_ores/hazards",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_ores/hazards.json",
          "text": "The RadAwayᵗᵐ pill",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.13.title",
          "label": "Hazardous Materials",
          "item_ref_count": 28,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 29
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_ores/hazards",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_ores/hazards.json",
          "text": "This is The Ultimate cure for ALL the hazards and the ONLY effective cure to Radiation. However, it is also the most Expensive and Difficult to make, requiring HV tier equipment and advanced materials.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.13.text",
          "label": "Hazardous Materials",
          "item_ref_count": 28,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 29
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_ores/hazards",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_ores/hazards.json",
          "text": "If all else fails, bringing along a bed to respawn is a good idea. If you don't have access to Wool or String, you can also make a Thatch Bed.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.14.text",
          "label": "Hazardous Materials",
          "item_ref_count": 28,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 29
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_ores/hazards",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_ores/hazards.json",
          "text": "Arsenicosis is an illness caused by inhaling Arsenic from its dust, vapor or Realgar. It slowly increases hunger, followed by nausea, and eventually death. The Face Mask and the Hazmat Suit provide protection from Arsenicosis.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.15.text",
          "label": "Hazardous Materials",
          "item_ref_count": 28,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 29
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_ores/hazards",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_ores/hazards.json",
          "text": "Asbestosis is an illness caused by inhaling Asbestos dust. If carried for long enough, it causes hunger. The Face Mask and the Hazmat Suit provide protection from Asbestosis.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.16.text",
          "label": "Hazardous Materials",
          "item_ref_count": 28,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 29
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_ores/hazards",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_ores/hazards.json",
          "text": "Berylliosis is an illness caused by skin contact with Beryllium dust or Liquid Beryllium. At first it might seem that it doesn't do much. However, the longer the exposure, the faster it kills you. Gloves and the Hazmat Suit provide protection from Berylliosis.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.17.text",
          "label": "Hazardous Materials",
          "item_ref_count": 28,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 29
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_ores/hazards",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_ores/hazards.json",
          "text": "Carbon Monoxide poisoning",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.18.title",
          "label": "Hazardous Materials",
          "item_ref_count": 28,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 29
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_ores/hazards",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_ores/hazards.json",
          "text": "Carbon Monoxide poisoning is an illness caused by inhaling Carbon Monoxide gas. Prolonged exposure causes nausea and quick death. The Face Mask and the Hazmat Suit provide protection from Carbon Monoxide.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.18.text",
          "label": "Hazardous Materials",
          "item_ref_count": 28,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 29
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_ores/hazards",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_ores/hazards.json",
          "text": "Carcinogens are substances that cause Cancer. They include Chromium Dust aswell as some organic compounds, such as Benzene and its products. Carcinogens don't go away on their own, but they give plenty of warnings before doing any real harm. It starts off with Weakness, followed by Hunger, then Slowness and later on Mining Fatique. Only when substantial enough exposure is accumulated it causes Death.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.19.text",
          "label": "Hazardous Materials",
          "item_ref_count": 28,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 29
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_ores/hazards",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_ores/hazards.json",
          "text": "The Face Mask protects from inhalation of harmful chemicals, while the Gloves protect from contact with Chromium Dust. The Hazmat Suit protects from both. In case of severe exposure, it is advised to take some Medicines to Prevent any possible damage.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.20.text",
          "label": "Hazardous Materials",
          "item_ref_count": 28,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 29
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_ores/hazards",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_ores/hazards.json",
          "text": "Chemical burns",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.21.title",
          "label": "Hazardous Materials",
          "item_ref_count": 28,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 29
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_ores/hazards",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_ores/hazards.json",
          "text": "Chemical burns are caused by inhalation or skin contact with certain Gases, Acids, Bases or Dusts. Exposure causes Weakness. The Face Mask protects from inhalation, while the Gloves protects from direct skin contact. The Hazmat Suit protects from everything.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.21.text",
          "label": "Hazardous Materials",
          "item_ref_count": 28,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 29
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_ores/hazards",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_ores/hazards.json",
          "text": "Irritation is caused by inhalation of Ammonium Formate or skin contact with Chromite Dust. Short contact only causes Weakness, but if ignored for longer it starts Damaging Randomly. The Face Mask protects from inhalation, while the Gloves protects from direct skin contact. The Hazmat Suit protects from both.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.22.text",
          "label": "Hazardous Materials",
          "item_ref_count": 28,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 29
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_ores/hazards",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_ores/hazards.json",
          "text": "Methanol poisoning",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.23.title",
          "label": "Hazardous Materials",
          "item_ref_count": 28,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 29
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_ores/hazards",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_ores/hazards.json",
          "text": "Methanol poisoning is caused by inhalation of Methanol. It causes a strong Blindness effect. The Face Mask and the Hazmat Suit protects from inhalation.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.23.text",
          "label": "Hazardous Materials",
          "item_ref_count": 28,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 29
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_ores/hazards",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_ores/hazards.json",
          "text": "Nauseating chemicals",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.24.title",
          "label": "Hazardous Materials",
          "item_ref_count": 28,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 29
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_ores/hazards",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_ores/hazards.json",
          "text": "Nauseating chemicals, such as Butyraldehyde, are substances that cause Nausea when inhaled. The Face Mask and the Hazmat Suit protect from inhalation.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.24.text",
          "label": "Hazardous Materials",
          "item_ref_count": 28,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 29
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_ores/hazards",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_ores/hazards.json",
          "text": "Poisonous materials",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.25.title",
          "label": "Hazardous Materials",
          "item_ref_count": 28,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 29
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_ores/hazards",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_ores/hazards.json",
          "text": "There are two types of Poisonous materials. Weak include Lead Dust, while the stronger poisons include Nitrogen Dioxide. Weak poisons cause a Progressively Worsening Poison effect, while Regular poisons cause Weakness and a Stronger Poison effect. The Face Mask protects from inhalation, while the Gloves protects from direct skin contact. The Hazmat Suit protects from any contact.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.25.text",
          "label": "Hazardous Materials",
          "item_ref_count": 28,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 29
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_ores/hazards",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_ores/hazards.json",
          "text": "Radioactive materials",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.26.title",
          "label": "Hazardous Materials",
          "item_ref_count": 28,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 29
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_ores/hazards",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_ores/hazards.json",
          "text": "Most Radioactive substances like Uranium 238 you will only meet at EV tier or above. They will Permanently Irradiate you. Small exposure causes glowing, but prolonged contact triggers a quick Death. We know it looks tasty and has a billion calories, but PLEASE DO NOT EAT URANIUM!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.26.text",
          "label": "Hazardous Materials",
          "item_ref_count": 28,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 29
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_ores/hazards",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_ores/hazards.json",
          "text": "Radioactive materials",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.27.title",
          "label": "Hazardous Materials",
          "item_ref_count": 28,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 29
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_ores/hazards",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_ores/hazards.json",
          "text": "Only the Hazmat Suit, as well as some advanced armors provide full protection from radiation. In the case you do get irradiated, only the RadAwayᵗᵐ pill can cure you.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.27.text",
          "label": "Hazardous Materials",
          "item_ref_count": 28,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 29
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_ores/hazards",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_ores/hazards.json",
          "text": "Silicosis is an illness caused by inhaling Silicon Dioxide Dust. Exposure causes a Reduction in both Health and the Air Supply you have while diving Under Water. The Face Mask and the Hazmat Suit provide protection from inhalation of silicon dioxide.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.28.text",
          "label": "Hazardous Materials",
          "item_ref_count": 28,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 29
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsstoneage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsstoneage.snbt",
          "text": "As you begin your journey, the first thing you'll notice is a completely changed world. You can no longer break trees with your fists, but fret not, for any caveman is not a real caveman without his stone tools!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "As you begin your journey, the first thing you'll notice is a completely changed world. You can no longer break trees with your fists, but fret not, for any caveman is not a real caveman without his stone tools!",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsstoneage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsstoneage.snbt",
          "text": "Instead of crafting a wooden pickaxe, you can just pick up rocks from the ground. Begin by collecting at least 4 rocks. You can collect rocks by breaking or right-clicking them with an empty hand.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "As you begin your journey, the first thing you'll notice is a completely changed world. You can no longer break trees with your fists, but fret not, for any caveman is not a real caveman without his stone tools!",

        {

          "label": "As you begin your journey, the first thing you'll notice is a completely changed world. You can no longer break trees with your fists, but fret not, for any caveman is not a real caveman without his stone tools!",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsstoneage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsstoneage.snbt",
          "text": "Find some Rocks",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "As you begin your journey, the first thing you'll notice is a completely changed world. You can no longer break trees with your fists, but fret not, for any caveman is not a real caveman without his stone tools!",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsstoneage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsstoneage.snbt",
          "text": "Thirst is represented by the blue bar above your hunger. You can and will die of dehydration if you dont keep your thirst meter filled! The easiest way to have a drink is by drinking from natural ponds in the wild, by right-clicking on them with an empty hand.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "As you begin your journey, the first thing you'll notice is a completely changed world. You can no longer break trees with your fists, but fret not, for any caveman is not a real caveman without his stone tools!",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsstoneage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsstoneage.snbt",
          "text": "To complete this quest, you need to drink a Potable source of water, can be regular or river water. You may need to drink multiple times so the quest completes properly",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "As you begin your journey, the first thing you'll notice is a completely changed world. You can no longer break trees with your fists, but fret not, for any caveman is not a real caveman without his stone tools!",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsstoneage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsstoneage.snbt",
          "text": "You don't have to worry about purifying water here, though Salt Water will just make you more thirsty.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "As you begin your journey, the first thing you'll notice is a completely changed world. You can no longer break trees with your fists, but fret not, for any caveman is not a real caveman without his stone tools!",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsstoneage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsstoneage.snbt",
          "text": "Remember to drink water!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "As you begin your journey, the first thing you'll notice is a completely changed world. You can no longer break trees with your fists, but fret not, for any caveman is not a real caveman without his stone tools!",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsstoneage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsstoneage.snbt",
          "text": "Drink from a Water Source with your Hand. (Not Salty or Hot)",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "As you begin your journey, the first thing you'll notice is a completely changed world. You can no longer break trees with your fists, but fret not, for any caveman is not a real caveman without his stone tools!",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsstoneage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsstoneage.snbt",
          "text": "Thirsty?",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "As you begin your journey, the first thing you'll notice is a completely changed world. You can no longer break trees with your fists, but fret not, for any caveman is not a real caveman without his stone tools!",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsstoneage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsstoneage.snbt",
          "text": "Another abundant item on the ground are sticks, which have many uses! They can create tools, torches, firestarters and more.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "As you begin your journey, the first thing you'll notice is a completely changed world. You can no longer break trees with your fists, but fret not, for any caveman is not a real caveman without his stone tools!",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsstoneage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsstoneage.snbt",
          "text": "To avoid having your inventory get clogged with different types of sticks, you can craft them into 'regular' sticks to stack them together. Later, you can bundle nine sticks into a Bunch, and the Bunch into a Bundle, which can then be used as a substitute for logs.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "As you begin your journey, the first thing you'll notice is a completely changed world. You can no longer break trees with your fists, but fret not, for any caveman is not a real caveman without his stone tools!",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsstoneage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsstoneage.snbt",
          "text": "You gotta handle that tool somehow",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "As you begin your journey, the first thing you'll notice is a completely changed world. You can no longer break trees with your fists, but fret not, for any caveman is not a real caveman without his stone tools!",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {

    {
      "id": "firmalife:drying",
      "label": "Drying",
      "origin": "namespace_generated",
      "confidence": 0.85,
      "support": 29,
      "evidence": [
        {
          "kind": "recipe_type",

        }
      ],
      "semantic_evidence": [
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/chocolate",
          "source": "jar:Firmalife-1.20.1-2.1.27.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/chocolate.json",
          "text": "Chocolate-making takes a few processing steps, for not much of a reward. It's important to remember, when playing Firmalife, that being a chocolatier is for your personal enjoyment and pleasure, rather than for trying to extract maximum value from any given input.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Chocolate",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/chocolate",
          "source": "jar:Firmalife-1.20.1-2.1.27.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/chocolate.json",
          "text": "To start chocolate processing, cocoa beans must first be roasted in an Oven to make Roasted Cocoa Beans. Then, craft the roasted beans with a Knife to split the beans into Cocoa Powder and Cocoa Butter.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Chocolate",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/chocolate",
          "source": "jar:Firmalife-1.20.1-2.1.27.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/chocolate.json",
          "text": "The Mixing Bowl is used to mix cocoa powder, butter, and sweetener (sugar or honey) to make Chocolate Blends. The ratio of cocoa butter to powder determines what comes out: - 1 Powder, 1 Butter, 1 Sweetener: Milk Chocolate - 2 Powder, 1 Sweetener: Dark Chocolate - 2 Butter, 1 Sweetener: White Chocolate",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Chocolate",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/chocolate",
          "source": "jar:Firmalife-1.20.1-2.1.27.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/chocolate.json",
          "text": "Finally, chocolate is dried on a Drying Mat to make Chocolate.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Chocolate",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/drying",
          "source": "jar:Firmalife-1.20.1-2.1.27.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/drying.json",
          "text": "The Drying Mat is used to dry items. It is made with Fruit Leaves, which are obtained from breaking the leaves of Fruit Trees.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Drying",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 8
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/drying",
          "source": "jar:Firmalife-1.20.1-2.1.27.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/drying.json",
          "text": "The recipe for the drying mat.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Drying",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 8
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/drying",
          "source": "jar:Firmalife-1.20.1-2.1.27.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/drying.json",
          "text": "To use the drying mat, place it out on the sun and add an item to it with . After a half day, it will be dried. If it rains, the drying process must start over.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Drying",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 8
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/drying",
          "source": "jar:Firmalife-1.20.1-2.1.27.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/drying.json",
          "text": "The solar drier functions the same as the drying mat, but 12x as fast.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Drying",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 8
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/drying",
          "source": "jar:Firmalife-1.20.1-2.1.27.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/drying.json",
          "text": "Drying mats can be automated. Pushing a piston head against a drying mat will pop the item off. Dropping an item onto a drying mat will place it on the mat.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Drying",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 8
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/drying",
          "source": "jar:Firmalife-1.20.1-2.1.27.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/drying.json",
          "text": "Drying fruit is a common use of the drying mat. Dried fruit is used in some recipes, and lasts longer.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Drying",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 8
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/drying",
          "source": "jar:Firmalife-1.20.1-2.1.27.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/drying.json",
          "text": "Tofu is made using a drying mat.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.6.text",
          "label": "Drying",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 8
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/drying",
          "source": "jar:Firmalife-1.20.1-2.1.27.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/drying.json",
          "text": "Cinnamon is made using a drying mat.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.7.text",
          "label": "Drying",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 8
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/more_fertilizer",
          "source": "jar:Firmalife-1.20.1-2.1.27.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/more_fertilizer.json",
          "text": "More Fertilizer Options",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "More Fertilizer Options",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 8
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/more_fertilizer",
          "source": "jar:Firmalife-1.20.1-2.1.27.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/more_fertilizer.json",
          "text": "Given a greater need for fertilization in Firmalife, there are more options for getting fertilizers.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "More Fertilizer Options",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 8
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/more_fertilizer",
          "source": "jar:Firmalife-1.20.1-2.1.27.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/more_fertilizer.json",
          "text": "Thatch can be Dried into Dry Grass, which can be used in a Composter as a brown item.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "More Fertilizer Options",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 8
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/more_fertilizer",
          "source": "jar:Firmalife-1.20.1-2.1.27.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/more_fertilizer.json",
          "text": "Compost Tumblers are a great way to produce more fertilizer. They must be connected to mechanical power in order to work. It can only be interacted with when not powered, so consider connecting it to a clutch!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "More Fertilizer Options",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 8
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/more_fertilizer",
          "source": "jar:Firmalife-1.20.1-2.1.27.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/more_fertilizer.json",
          "text": "The compost tumbler is unique in that it takes more types of compost, and does not require precise ratios in order to work.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "More Fertilizer Options",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 8
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/more_fertilizer",
          "source": "jar:Firmalife-1.20.1-2.1.27.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/more_fertilizer.json",
          "text": "The tumbler can take green and brown items like a regular composter. It can also take pottery sherds, charcoal, fish, and bones in small amounts.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "More Fertilizer Options",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 8
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/more_fertilizer",
          "source": "jar:Firmalife-1.20.1-2.1.27.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/more_fertilizer.json",
          "text": "Smashing pottery with a hammer yields sherds.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "More Fertilizer Options",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 8
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/more_fertilizer",
          "source": "jar:Firmalife-1.20.1-2.1.27.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/more_fertilizer.json",
          "text": "Green and brown items count the same, being on the range 1-4, but the new additions like fish always count for 1. Adding too much weird stuff to the composter causes it to produce rotten compost. Further, you will not know it is rotten until the very end! If the compost is more than fifteen percent bones, fish, or pottery, or more than twenty percent charcoal. it will rot. Or, if there are 10 or more green units than brown units, it will rot.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.6.text",
          "label": "More Fertilizer Options",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 8
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/more_fertilizer",
          "source": "jar:Firmalife-1.20.1-2.1.27.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/more_fertilizer.json",
          "text": "Favorable amounts of certain additions can extend or shorten the length of time it takes for the compost to complete. Play around with it and see what happens. If 32 units are in the composter, 3 compost will be produced. If at least 24, 2 compost will be made. If 16 or more, 1 will be made. Below that, and there will be no compost.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.7.text",
          "label": "More Fertilizer Options",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 8
        }
      ],
      "aliases": []
    },
