          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel.json",
          "text": "Cryo Freezer",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.title",
          "label": "Better Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel.json",
          "text": "Fuel is hard to get; it requires venturing out to the ocean, looking for oil spouts. The Cryo Freezer introduces a new way to obtain a better fuel - Cryo Fuel. Cryo Fuel is much more efficient than refined fuel, allowing you to launch a rocket with just one bucket.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Better Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel.json",
          "text": "It's created by collecting ice shards, which are found as an ore underground on cold planets. The ice shard then needs to be inserted into a Cryo Freezer, and it will convert to 25 mB of Cryo Fuel. Alternatively, you can convert ice, packed ice and blue ice to fuel. Once you've made at least two buckets (one for a launch and the other for a return trip), place the buckets in the rocket fuel slot.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Better Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel.json",
          "text": "Cryo Fuel",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.title",
          "label": "Better Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel.json",
          "text": "In addition to being an excellent fuel, cryo fuel has some other features. When placed, it will damage anything inside, similar to lava. It also freezes water, allowing for an infinite ice source.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Better Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/detection",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/detection.json",
          "text": "Oxygen Sensor",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.title",
          "label": "Detection",
          "item_ref_count": 3,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/detection",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/detection.json",
          "text": "The oxygen sensor is a block that will emit a redstone signal when it detects oxygen. You can shift-right-click it to invert the signal. Despite its name, you can also change its detection type by right-clicking with a wrench. It has the option to detect oxygen, safe temperature, and normal gravity.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Detection",
          "item_ref_count": 3,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/energizer",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/energizer.json",
          "text": "The energizer is a machine capable of powering items and storing large amounts of energy. To charge an item, power the energizer and then right-click on the energizer with that item. It stores 2 million energy, making it capable of charging machines for a very long time. It also retains its energy when broken.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Energizer",
          "item_ref_count": 3,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/flags",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/flags.json",
          "text": "Flags are decorative blocks available in 16 colors. By default, when placed, they will display the face of the flag owner. They can be configured to display a custom image from a URL by right-clicking and entering the URL in the GUI. This will display the internet image onto the flag.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Flags",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",

          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/fluid_pipes.json",
          "text": "Fluid Pipes",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Fluid Pipes",

        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/fluid_pipes",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/fluid_pipes.json",
          "text": "Fluid Pipes",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.title",
          "label": "Fluid Pipes",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,

        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/fluid_pipes",

          "text": "Fluid pipes are similar to cables but with fluid transfer instead of energy transfer. To use them, craft some fluid pipes and a wrench. Then, right-click on one of the pipes with the wrench to cycle through the pipe's modes: 'none,' 'normal,' 'insert,' and 'extract.'",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Fluid Pipes",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/fluid_pipes",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/fluid_pipes.json",
          "text": "Pipes with 'insert' set will receive fluid from the network, while pipes with 'extract' set will pump fluid into the network.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Fluid Pipes",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/fluid_pipes",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/fluid_pipes.json",
          "text": "Like cables, fluid pipes also have a duct block for transfering fluids safely in and out of sealed structures.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Fluid Pipes",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 4
        },

          "text": "Once you've got energy, you'll want to craft a compressor. This machine can compress ingots and blocks into plates, which are needed to craft many things.",

          "label": "Creating Plates",
          "item_ref_count": 3,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/fuel.json",
          "text": "Even after you place your rocket, you will not be able to fly just yet. To fly, you will need to fill the rocket with fuel.",

          "label": "Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 6,

        },

          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/fuel.json",

          "text": "Oil is found in large amounts spouting over the ocean surface. Collect as much oil as you can, as oil is non-renewable, and you need a lot of it for each rocket launch.",

          "text": "After collecting enough oil (at least six buckets for a launch and return), you will need to refine the oil into fuel. To do this, you will need to craft a fuel refinery. Power it, then place the oil in the left slot, and it will be refined into fuel.",

          "text": "Clay is an incredibly useful and balanced material which can be used for pottery. It can prove challenging to locate at first. Clay is usually hidden by grass, but it is often found in two locations. In areas with at least 175mm Annual Rainfall, clay can be found in patches all over the place, usually marked by the presence of certain Plants.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Pottery",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 22
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery.json",
          "text": "Clay Indicators",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.name",
          "label": "Pottery",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 22
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery.json",
          "text": "Clay, with one of the plants that may indicate its presence.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Pottery",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 22
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery.json",
          "text": "Athyrium Fern, Canna, Goldenrod, Pampas Grass, Perovskia, and Water Canna all indicate the presence of clay nearby. Clay can also be found in smaller deposits close to water sources, such as rivers, lakes, or ponds. Like with rocks, clay can be knapped to form new items. It requires five clay in your hand to knap. Unlike rocks, if you make a mistake, you can simply close the knapping interface, reshape your clay, and try again.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Pottery",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 22
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery.json",
          "text": "The Knapping Interface.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Pottery",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 22
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery.json",
          "text": "The small vessel is one such item. Like all pottery items, it must be fired before it can be used. Firing is a process of heating the item up to a point where the clay will turn into a hard Ceramic material, which requires heating to 1400 °C, or Yellow White. In order to do this in the early game, you will need to use a Pit Kiln.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Pottery",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 22
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery.json",
          "text": "Small Vessel",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.title",
          "label": "Pottery",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 22
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery.json",
          "text": "Knapping a Clay Small Vessel.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Pottery",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 22
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery.json",
          "text": "Another useful pottery item is the Jug. It can be used to pick up and drink fluids, such as fresh water. In order to use it, simply the jug on the fluid in the world. Then use the jug in order to drink from it. The jug can hold 100 mB of fluid at a time.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.6.text",
          "label": "Pottery",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 22
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery.json",
          "text": "Knapping a Clay Jug.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.7.text",
          "label": "Pottery",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 22
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery.json",
          "text": "Clay is also necessary for making Molds. Molds can have molten metal poured into them, which will eventually solidify into the shape of a mold. The item and potentially the mold can then be retrieved by using on the mold. The most simple type of mold is the ingot mold, to the right.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.8.text",
          "label": "Pottery",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 22
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery.json",
          "text": "Knapping a Clay Ingot Mold.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.9.text",
          "label": "Pottery",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 22
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery.json",
          "text": "The mold then needs to be fired, like all clay items, to be usable - likely in a Pit Kiln. Once it is fired, molten metal can be poured in. Once the metal has cooled enough, it can be extracted.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.10.text",
          "label": "Pottery",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 22
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery.json",
          "text": "The next few pages show several of the knapping patterns for various tools.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.11.text",
          "label": "Pottery",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 22
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery.json",
          "text": "A Prospector's Pick is an essential tool for locating large quantities of ore.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.12.text",
          "label": "Pottery",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 22
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery.json",
          "text": "A Pickaxe! The bread and butter tool for mining.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.13.text",
          "label": "Pottery",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 22
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery.json",
          "text": "A Saw is a tool which is required in order to craft advanced wooden components like a Workbench along with many other devices like Supports.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.14.text",
          "label": "Pottery",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 22
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery.json",
          "text": "A Scythe is a tool that can harvest plants and leaves in a 3x3x3 area!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.15.text",
          "label": "Pottery",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 22
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery.json",
          "text": "A Chisel is a tool used for smoothing blocks as well as creating a large number of decorative blocks.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.16.text",
          "label": "Pottery",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 22
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery.json",
          "text": "An Axe for all your tree chopping purposes. Note that stone axes are less efficient than metal!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.17.text",
          "label": "Pottery",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 22
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery.json",
          "text": "A Hammer is an essential tool to create and work on Anvils.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.18.text",
          "label": "Pottery",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 22
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery.json",
          "text": "A Knife can be used as a weapon, or as a cutting tool for plant type blocks.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.19.text",
          "label": "Pottery",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 22
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery.json",
          "text": "A Hoe used for planting and maintaining Crops.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.20.text",
          "label": "Pottery",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 22
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery.json",
          "text": "A Shovel for all your digging purposes.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.21.text",
          "label": "Pottery",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 22
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/buildinggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/buildinggadget.json",
          "text": "Building Gadget",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Building Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/buildinggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/buildinggadget.json",
          "text": "The building gadget makes building a large number of blocks all at once extremely easy! First, ensure the gadget has some energy. Second, Shift right-click a block in the world to specify the block to build with.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Building Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/buildinggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/buildinggadget.json",
          "text": "You can choose your Building Mode by holding the settings key. Each mode is described in its appropriate entry in this book. The default mode is Build to Me. When looking at a block in the world, you'll see a ghostly image of the blocks that will be placed. If you're missing the required items or energy to place these blocks, they will have a red overlay. Right click to place the blocks as shown.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Building Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/buildinggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/buildinggadget.json",
          "text": "Build Preview",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.title",
          "label": "Building Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/buildinggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/buildinggadget.json",
          "text": "Seeing a preview of what will be built",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Building Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/buildinggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/buildinggadget.json",
          "text": "When you right click, building will begin. Items will be taken out of your inventory to be placed into the world, and any blocks you don't have the necessary item for will be skipped. Don't forget you can undo if you make a mistake!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Building Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "The copy paste gadget makes replicating structures far easier! To start, ensure you're in 'copy' mode in the settings menu. Right click one corner of a structure,and then shift-right click an opposing corner. If done correctly, a green outline will surround your structure.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "Copying a structure",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "Switch the gadget to 'paste' mode, and you'll see a ghostly preview of the structure you've copied. The block you right clicked during copying is the starting point of the structure. To place the structure in world, simply right click! All items necessary for building will be removed from your inventory, and any blocks you don't have items for will be skipped.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "Paste Preview",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.title",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "Previewing a paste",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "To see a list of the items you need, open the settings menu and choose the 'material list' option. This is a scrollable list showing what items you do or do not have, and can be sorted in multiple ways. Try it out!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "Material List",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.title",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "Needed items",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "Its possible to make fine tuned adjustments to the copy area by opening the Adjust Placement window in the settings menu. From here, you can adjust the position of the start block and end block. Click the plus and minus buttons to see the green box change in real time. You can also swap to 'absolute' coordinates mode and enter in-game world coordinates instead.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.6.text",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "Adjusting Copy",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.7.title",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "Changing the area you are copying",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.7.text",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "Theres a similar option for paste mode. In this UI, you'll be adjusting the location of the starting block. Click the buttons and the render should update in-world in real time.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.8.text",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "Adjusting Paste",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.9.title",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "Changing the location of the paste",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.9.text",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste.json",
          "text": "Cut Paste Gadget",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Cut Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste.json",
          "text": "The cut paste gadget makes moving structures a breeze, and works just like the Copy Paste Gadget! To start, ensure you're in 'cut' mode in the settings menu. Right click one corner of a structure,and then shift-right click an opposing corner. If done correctly, a red outline will surround your structure.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Cut Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste.json",
          "text": "Cutting a structure",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Cut Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste.json",
          "text": "The structure will not be removed until you click the 'cut' button in the settings menu. Switch the gadget to 'paste' mode, and you'll see a ghostly preview of the structure you've copied. To place the structure in world, simply right click! No items are necessary for this, as its using the blocks you removed earlier.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Cut Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste.json",
          "text": "Paste Preview",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.title",
          "label": "Cut Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste.json",
          "text": "Previewing a paste",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Cut Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste.json",
          "text": "You can only have one structure stored on the gadget at a time. Placing it in the world will erased the stored information on the gadget. Be careful when pasting the structure, you only get one chance, exchange mode is recommended! If you attempt to cut a new area while data already exists, you'll be prompted to confirm. If you do this, the first set of data will be lost forever!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Cut Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste.json",
          "text": "All block-entity data will be moved along with the structure, including the contents of chests and block configurations. Much like the Copy Paste Gadget, you can modify the cut or paste positions with the appropriate settings menu option. See that chapter for details!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Cut Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/destruction",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/destruction.json",
          "text": "Destruction Gadget",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Destruction Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 3
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/destruction",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/destruction.json",
          "text": "The destruction gadget makes clearing a large area very easy. Any blocks cleared will be voided!. First, open the settings menu and adjust the area. This area is with respect to the block (and block face) you are looking at. If you look at the top face of a block, 'up' will be relative to the player's screen.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Destruction Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 3
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/destruction",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/destruction.json",
          "text": "Destruction Gadget",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.title",
          "label": "Destruction Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 3
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/destruction",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/destruction.json",
          "text": "Destroying an area, click the arrow to see diffent pictures!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Destruction Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 3
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/destruction",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/destruction.json",
          "text": "Once you're satisfied with the settings, right click on a block to void all the highlighted blocks. They will be removed from the world. You may undo this action if you make a mistake. It will not remove items from your inventory to undo, as the gadget is just returning the voided blocks to the world.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Destruction Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 3
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget.json",
          "text": "Exchanging Gadget",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Exchanging Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget.json",
          "text": "The exchanging gadget can swap one set of blocks for another! It cannot, however, replace air blocks. Use a Building Gadget for that! First, ensure the gadget has some energy. Second, Shift right-click a block in the world to specify the block to exchange to.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Exchanging Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget.json",
          "text": "You can choose your Exchanging Mode by holding the settings key. Each mode is described in its appropriate entry in this book. The default mode is Surface. When looking at a block in the world, you'll see a ghostly image of the blocks that will be placed over top of existing blocks. If you're missing the required items or energy to place these blocks, they will have a red overlay. Right click to exchange the blocks as shown.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Exchanging Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget.json",
          "text": "Exchange Preview",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.title",
          "label": "Exchanging Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget.json",
          "text": "Seeing a preview of what will be exchanged",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Exchanging Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget.json",
          "text": "When you right click, exchanging will begin. Items will be taken out of your inventory to be placed into the world, and any blocks you don't have the necessary item for will be skipped. Items for the removed blocks will be returned to you. Note: You can enchant this tool with silk touch. Without it grass will return dirt, etc.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Exchanging Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 5
        }
      ],
      "aliases": []
    },
    {
      "id": "tfc:wild/crops",
      "label": "Wild Crops",

      ],
      "semantic_evidence": [
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_crops",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_crops.json",
          "text": "Wild Crops",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Wild Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 20,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_crops",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_crops.json",
          "text": "Wild Crops can be found scattered around the world, growing in small patches. They can be harvested for food and seeds, which can then be cultivated themselves in the not-wild form. Harvesting wild crops can be done with your fists, or with a Knife or other sharp tool. When broken, they will drop Seeds and some Products.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Wild Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 20,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_crops",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_crops.json",
          "text": "Wild Wheat",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.name",
          "label": "Wild Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 20,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_crops",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_crops.json",
          "text": "An example of a wild crop, in this case Wheat.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Wild Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 20,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_crops",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_crops.json",
          "text": "Every crop that can be cultivated can also be found in the wild. Wild crops will look similar to their cultivated counterparts, but are more hidden within the grass. Wild crops are only mature from June to October. Otherwise, they appear dead until the next Summer.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Wild Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 20,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_crops",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_crops.json",
          "text": "All different varieties of wild crop",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Wild Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 20,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_crops",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_crops.json",
          "text": "Wild crops will spawn in climates near where the crop itself can be cultivated, so if looking for a specific crop, look in the climate where the crop can be cultivated. However, unlike Crops that the player has planted, wild crops do not require Hydration. Instead, they are found in areas depending on the average Temperature and Rainfall.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Wild Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 20,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_crops",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_crops.json",
          "text": "Finding Wild Crops",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.title",
          "label": "Wild Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 20,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_crops",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/wild_crops.json",
          "text": "The next pages show a table of the environments where wild crops can be found.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",

          "item_ref_count": 20,

        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "create:ponder/postbox",
          "source": "file:minecraft/lang-overlays",
          "text": "Just like trains, Postboxes maintain their behaviour in unloaded chunks",

          "count": 11
        },

          "text": "Right-click a Train Station and place the Postbox nearby",

          "source": "jar:create-1.20.1-6.0.8.jar!assets/*/lang/en_us.json",
          "text": "→ Warehouse",
          "key": "lang-ponder:create.ponder.postbox.text_10",
          "label": "Transporting packages between Postboxes",
          "count": 11
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "create:ponder/postbox",
          "source": "jar:create-1.20.1-6.0.8.jar!assets/*/lang/en_us.json",
          "text": "Just like trains, Postboxes maintain their behaviour in unloaded chunks",
          "key": "lang-ponder:create.ponder.postbox.text_11",
          "label": "Transporting packages between Postboxes",
          "count": 11
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "create:ponder/postbox",
          "source": "jar:create-1.20.1-6.0.8.jar!assets/*/lang/en_us.json",
          "text": "Packages can still be delivered from or to their inventory",
          "key": "lang-ponder:create.ponder.postbox.text_12",
          "label": "Transporting packages between Postboxes",
          "count": 11
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "create:ponder/postbox",
          "source": "jar:create-1.20.1-6.0.8.jar!assets/*/lang/en_us.json",
          "text": "Assign it an address in the inventory UI",
          "key": "lang-ponder:create.ponder.postbox.text_2",
          "label": "Transporting packages between Postboxes",
          "count": 11
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "create:ponder/postbox",
          "source": "jar:create-1.20.1-6.0.8.jar!assets/*/lang/en_us.json",
          "text": "If the address of an inserted package does not match it..",
          "key": "lang-ponder:create.ponder.postbox.text_3",
          "label": "Transporting packages between Postboxes",
          "count": 11
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "create:ponder/postbox",
          "source": "jar:create-1.20.1-6.0.8.jar!assets/*/lang/en_us.json",

          "count": 11
        },
        {

          "source": "jar:create-1.20.1-6.0.8.jar!assets/*/lang/en_us.json",

          "label": "Transporting packages between Postboxes",
          "count": 11
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "create:ponder/postbox",
          "source": "jar:create-1.20.1-6.0.8.jar!assets/*/lang/en_us.json",
